独立评测发现

来源：独立评审 · 口径 v2 · 源 Run 39502979147 / attempt 1

最新上游状态：未复核。原评审“已解决”不作为上游修复证明；应用绕行、业务 QA 通过均不关闭框架问题。

等级与类型怎么定
  • 严重阻塞交付，或造成数据丢失、安全问题，且没有合理绕行
  • 重要按指引或公开 API 正常使用会静默得到错误结果，或只能用非公开手段绕行
  • 轻微能完成，但要多花排查成本或在应用侧绕行
  • 信息措辞、示例完整度等不影响结果的改进
  • 实现缺陷实现违背文档或公开约定
  • 能力缺口需求属于框架职责，但缺少能力或公开接入
  • 指引错误指引、示例或注释写错，或彼此矛盾，照做会出错
  • 指引缺失指引缺少必要说明或示例，需要读源码或试探
  • 易用性改进现有能力可用，但有改进空间

NocoBase3 库、插件、模板与指引 3

重要能力缺口上游未复核候选反馈：证据不足原评审置信度：confirmed
F1 · @nocobase/db 未公开唯一约束冲突识别，Repository 普通写入泄漏驱动错误

NocoBase3 内核 · @nocobase/db · @nocobase/repository-input · packages/@nocobase/db/skills/nocobase-db/SKILL.md · 原评审：未解决

⌄

反馈证据核对

定向复核未完成：Pi invocation timed out after 180 seconds.

    反馈复核是对原始反馈的证据核对，不等于人工确认、上游修复状态或自动修复授权；保留原评审置信度、分类与处理状态。

    db 包内部已有健壮的 isUniqueConstraintViolation，覆盖 Oracle -6602、PostgreSQL 23505、MySQL ER_DUP_ENTRY 和 SQLite 错误码，并沿 cause/originalError 链查找；但 db/dist/src/index.d.ts 与 index.js 没有导出该函数。Repository 的 createOne/update 路径不把唯一冲突归一化为 RepositoryError；repository-input 的错误目录只有 FIELD_NOT_FOUND、RECORD_NOT_FOUND、SCOPE_VIOLATION 等，没有 UNIQUE_CONFLICT/DUPLICATE。设备应用只能复制驱动码判断，并在 service 中先查后写。

    实际影响
    应用为一条业务唯一约束就要维护跨数据库驱动码副本；预检查在并发创建下存在竞态窗口，两个同 code 请求仍可能同时通过预检查，最终一个请求收到未归一化的驱动错误而不是稳定的 409/ALREADY_EXISTS。
    建议改法
    从 @nocobase/db 公开 isUniqueConstraintViolation 或更高层的 normalizeRepositoryError；在 repository-input 增加 UNIQUE_CONFLICT（映射 ALREADY_EXISTS/409），让 Repository createOne/update 在写路径统一转换唯一冲突；在 db Skill 增加“业务唯一约束 → 统一冲突错误”的 recipe。
    诊断 · 触发条件、应有行为、绕行代价与验收标准
    触发条件
    迁移对 devices.code 建唯一索引；应用通过 Repository 创建或更新同 code 设备，且未在应用层复制驱动错误映射。
    应有能力 / 行为
    Repository 将数据库唯一冲突归一化为公开的冲突错误，路由可直接映射为 HTTP 409 和稳定 reason；并发创建也返回同一契约。
    实际观察
    Repository 普通写入把驱动原始错误抛给上层；db 未导出唯一约束分类器，repository-input 错误目录也没有唯一冲突类别。
    应用绕行与代价
    应用复制 isUniqueConstraintViolation 的驱动码判断，在 service 中先查询 code 再写入，并在 catch 中把自定义错误转成 DeviceCodeTakenError。
    改进后的验收标准
    两个并发请求创建同一 code 时，一个成功，另一个稳定返回 409 和 ALREADY_EXISTS 或公开的 UNIQUE_CONFLICT reason；应用无需导入或复制任何数据库驱动错误码。
    评审时证据 · 冻结快照 9 条
    packages/@nocobase/db/dist/src/database/internal/unique-constraint.js:1–24E25

    内部唯一约束分类器覆盖 Oracle、PostgreSQL、MySQL、SQLite 错误码，并沿 cause/originalError 链查找且防循环。

    export function isUniqueConstraintViolation(error) {
    const visited = new Set();
    let current = error;
    while (current && typeof current === 'object' && !visited.has(current)) {
        visited.add(current);
        const record = current;
        const code = record.code;
        const number = record.errno ?? record.number ?? record.errorNum;
        if (record.errCode === -6602 ||
            code === '23505' ||
            code === 'ER_DUP_ENTRY' ||
            code === 'SQLITE_CONSTRAINT' ||
            code === 'SQLITE_CONSTRAINT_UNIQUE' ||
            code === 'SQLITE_CONSTRAINT_PRIMARYKEY' ||
            number === 1 ||
            number === 1062 ||
            number === 2601 ||
            number === 2627) {
            return true;
        }
        current = record.cause ?? record.originalError;
    }
    return false;
}

    packages/@nocobase/db/dist/src/repository/internal/knex-execution-adapter.js:540–562E27

    upsert 路径使用唯一约束分类器识别冲突，并在冲突后重新查询现有行，避免重复插入。

            return this.inTransaction((adapter) => adapter.executeUpsertOne(plan));
    }
    async executeUpsertOne(plan) {
        const selected = await this.lockByUnique(plan.collection, plan.by);
        if (selected === 'missing') {
            try {
                return await this.inSavepoint((adapter) => adapter.executeCreateOne({
                    collection: plan.collection,
                    fields: plan.fields,
                    values: plan.createValues,
                    relations: plan.createRelations,
                    select: plan.select,
                    scopeCheck: plan.createScopeCheck,
                    relationScopes: plan.createRelationScopes,
                }));
            }
            catch (error) {
                if (!isUniqueConstraintViolation(error))
                    throw error;
                const concurrent = await this.lockByUnique(plan.collection, plan.by);
                if (concurrent === 'missing')
                    throw error;
            }

    export const repositoryErrorStatuses = Object.freeze({
    // The request names something that does not exist or cannot be used that way.
    FIELD_NOT_FOUND: 'INVALID_ARGUMENT',
    FIELD_CAPABILITY_NOT_SUPPORTED: 'INVALID_ARGUMENT',
    FIELD_NOT_WRITABLE: 'INVALID_ARGUMENT',
    RELATION_NOT_FOUND: 'INVALID_ARGUMENT',
    READ_ONLY_COLLECTION: 'INVALID_ARGUMENT',
    INVALID_AST: 'INVALID_ARGUMENT',
    INVALID_CONTEXT: 'INVALID_ARGUMENT',
    INVALID_FILTER: 'INVALID_ARGUMENT',
    INVALID_PAGINATION: 'INVALID_ARGUMENT',
    INVALID_SELECT: 'INVALID_ARGUMENT',
    INVALID_SORT: 'INVALID_ARGUMENT',
    INVALID_AGGREGATE: 'INVALID_ARGUMENT',
    INVALID_GROUP_BY: 'INVALID_ARGUMENT',
    INVALID_DISTINCT: 'INVALID_ARGUMENT',
    INVALID_STREAM: 'INVALID_ARGUMENT',
    INVALID_MUTATION: 'INVALID_ARGUMENT',
    INVALID_UNIQUE_SELECTOR: 'INVALID_ARGUMENT',
    VARIABLE_NOT_FOUND: 'INVALID_ARGUMENT',
    MUTATION_LIMIT_EXCEEDED: 'INVALID_ARGUMENT',
    RELATION_ACTION_NOT_ALLOWED: 'INVALID_ARGUMENT',
    // A target the request body refers to is missing, or outside the relation's scope, which reads the same so that
    // forbidden and absent stay indistinguishable. The body named it, so this is a bad request rather than a 404.
    RELATION_TARGET_NOT_FOUND: 'INVALID_ARGUMENT',
    // The record the operation addresses does not exist, or is outside the caller's scope.
    RECORD_NOT_FOUND: 'NOT_FOUND',
    // SCOPE_VIOLATION is PERMISSION_DENIED rather than NOT_FOUND because the caller can see the record; it was their own
    // values that pushed it out of scope. Saying so leaks nothing about anyone else's data, and the request cannot be
    // repaired without being told. A scope that simply does not match is a different thing: it is RECORD_NOT_FOUND or an
    // empty result, so that forbidden and absent stay indistinguishable.
    READ_FORBIDDEN: 'PERMISSION_DENIED',
    FIELD_READ_FORBIDDEN: 'PERMISSION_DENIED',
    RELATION_READ_FORBIDDEN: 'PERMISSION_DENIED',
    WRITE_FORBIDDEN: 'PERMISSION_DENIED',
    FIELD_WRITE_FORBIDDEN: 'PERMISSION_DENIED',
    RELATION_WRITE_FORBIDDEN: 'PERMISSION_DENIED',
    SCOPE_VIOLATION: 'PERMISSION_DENIED',
    // The data changed or matched differently from what the request assumed.
    VERSION_CONFLICT: 'ABORTED',
    MULTIPLE_RECORDS_MATCHED: 'ABORTED',
    MULTIPLE_RELATION_TARGETS_MATCHED: 'ABORTED',
    RELATION_UPSERT_TARGET_OUTSIDE_SCOPE: 'ABORTED',
    RECORD_OUTSIDE_SCOPE: 'ABORTED',
    RELATION_REASSIGNMENT_REQUIRED: 'ABORTED',
    // Policies, write policies and Collections are server-owned, so one that is missing or cannot be built is a
    // misconfiguration rather than something the caller got wrong. The rest are server-side defects.
    COLLECTION_NOT_FOUND: 'INTERNAL',
    INVALID_WRITE_POLICY: 'INTERNAL',
    INVALID_POLICY: 'INTERNAL',
    POLICY_REQUIRED: 'INTERNAL',
    INVALID_STORED_VALUE: 'INTERNAL',
    QUERY_ALREADY_CONSUMED: 'INTERNAL',
    QUERY_TRANSACTION_COMPLETED: 'INTERNAL',
    REPOSITORY_EVENT_RECURSION: 'INTERNAL',
});
    packages/@nocobase/db/skills/nocobase-db/SKILL.md:137–149E29

    db Skill 推荐使用 Repository 进行查询，并给出过滤条件入口。

    The rules are absolute, because these files are history that has already run on other machines:

- **Self-contained.** Spell out every table, field, index, constraint and metadata operation in the file. Never import a Collection definition, model, registry or shared constant that keeps evolving — that silently changes both the behavior and the checksum of something already applied.
- **Immutable once merged.** Check with `git log -- <file>` if unsure. Before the branch is merged you may correct the file in place; after it, every change is a new, later file. Never hard-code a previous checksum to make an edited migration look untouched. Correcting it in place changes nothing on its own — it is recorded as executed, so a plain apply skips it and the database keeps the schema the old file produced. `pnpm nocobase db redo` rolls the latest batch back and applies it again.
- **The file name body matches `name`**, and the name is unique across every source in the application.
- **Declare `irreversible: true`** when there is no real reverse operation, instead of writing a `down()` that quietly does nothing.
- **A seed is idempotent**, keyed on a stable business value backed by a unique constraint, so a repeated run is a no-op — `upsertOne` is the direct way to express that. A seed context has no `builder` at all, and a seed has no rollback or truncate behavior.

A backfill belongs in the migration that makes it necessary, using `query` from the same context — not in a seed.

Sample data goes in the same `seeds` directory, declared with `defineSeed({ name, sample: true, run })`. It runs only when the application installs its database with `app.sampleData` set (`APP_SAMPLE_DATA=true`): the connection held no migration or seed history before that start, or `db reset` rebuilt it. Otherwise the seed is recorded as skipped and never runs on its own; `pnpm nocobase db sample` runs every skipped sample seed in development, and a deployment refuses it. Sample data that has to go through other plugins' services, such as projects with issues, is registered instead on `sampleDataToken` from `@nocobase/app-server/sample-data`, from a service provider's `boot()`: `container.resolve(sampleDataToken).register({ name, packageName, run })`. It runs once the application is ready, under the same condition, and is recorded in the default connection's seed history as `sample-data:<name>`. The seed history's `status` column, `executed` or `skipped`, records which happened; the library adds it to an existing history table on the next run.

Both run inside a transaction by default. `transaction: false` opts out, and a failure then leaves partial work behind with no history record, so take it only when the operation genuinely cannot run in one.
    packages/@nocobase/db/skills/nocobase-db/SKILL.md:246–258E30

    db Skill 说明迁移和 upsert 的使用方式，但没有把唯一冲突转业务错误的公开 recipe。

    Resolve the manager once — in a route factory, or as a constructor dependency of a service — not inside every handler. `db.repository()`, `db.query()`, `db.builder()`, `db.collections()` and `db.transaction()` take the connection name as their last argument and use the default connection when it is omitted, so single-connection code never names one.

`filter`, `values`, `select` and `sort` each accept a builder callback or the equivalent serializable JSON AST, and mean the same thing either way. They do not accept a shorthand string array such as `['id', 'name']` or `['-createdAt']`. Field names are Collection logical names, never column names. A write returns a result object — the record is `result.record`, alongside `result.version` and the created relation targets — rather than the record itself. `findMany` is awaited for an array or iterated asynchronously to consume a large result incrementally.

The methods are `findOne`, `findMany`, `count`, `exists`, `aggregate`, `groupBy`, `createOne`, `createMany`, `updateOne`, `updateMany`, `upsertOne`, `deleteOne` and `deleteMany`; `describeMutation` and `validateMutation` report capabilities and pre-validate input, and `withPolicy` returns a scoped Repository.

Boundaries that are easy to get wrong:

- `findOne` takes the first match; `updateOne` and `deleteOne` require exactly one; `upsertOne` requires a unique equality condition.
- `updateMany` and `deleteMany` refuse an empty scope. Pass `all: true` only when the task explicitly calls for every row — never to make an error go away.
- `createMany` and `updateMany` do not accept nested relation writes. `createOne` accepts `create` and `connect` on a relation, not the full set.
- `disconnect` detaches; `delete` removes the target record. A relation target update or delete stays inside the current parent record's scope.
- Repository does not apply the application's permissions. A route handler decides what the caller may see and change _before_ anything reaches `filter`, `values` or `select`. `writePolicy` defaults to `true` for an internal Repository and `false` for one reached from an API route, so a route-facing Repository needs an explicit allow list.
    app/server/providers/devices.ts:84–119E32

    应用复制跨数据库唯一约束判断，覆盖 PostgreSQL、MySQL、SQLite 等驱动码，说明公开入口缺失导致业务侧重复实现。

    /**
 * Mirrors the database drivers' unique-constraint signals. The Repository exposes no duplicate-code error of its own,
 * so the service checks the business key first and keeps this as the race-safe fallback.
 */
function isUniqueConstraintViolation(error: unknown): boolean {
  const visited = new Set<object>();
  let current: unknown = error;

  while (
    typeof current === 'object' &&
    current !== null &&
    !visited.has(current)
  ) {
    visited.add(current);
    const record = current as Record<string, unknown>;
    const code = record.code;
    const number = record.errno ?? record.number ?? record.errorNum;
    if (
      record.errCode === -6602 ||
      code === '23505' ||
      code === 'ER_DUP_ENTRY' ||
      code === 'SQLITE_CONSTRAINT' ||
      code === 'SQLITE_CONSTRAINT_UNIQUE' ||
      code === 'SQLITE_CONSTRAINT_PRIMARYKEY' ||
      number === 1 ||
      number === 1062 ||
      number === 2601 ||
      number === 2627
    ) {
      return true;
    }
    current = record.cause ?? record.originalError;
  }

  return false;
}
    app/server/providers/devices.ts:151–175E33

    应用在 create/update 中先查询 code 是否已存在，再写入并在 catch 中兜底转换冲突。

        async create(input) {
      if (await codeIsTaken(input.code)) {
        throw new DeviceCodeTakenError();
      }

      const now = new Date();
      try {
        const result = await devices.createOne({
          values: {
            id: randomUUID(),
            code: input.code,
            name: input.name,
            status: input.status,
            createdAt: now,
            updatedAt: now,
          },
        });
        return toView(result.record);
      } catch (error) {
        if (isUniqueConstraintViolation(error)) {
          throw new DeviceCodeTakenError();
        }
        throw error;
      }
    },

    迁移为 devices.code 建立 uq_devices_code 唯一索引，数据库层确实拒绝重复 code。

        await builder.createCollection('devices', (collection) => {
      collection.string('id', { length: 36 }).notNull();
      collection.string('code', { length: 64 }).notNull();
      collection.string('name', { length: 255 }).notNull();
      collection.string('status', { length: 32 }).notNull();
      collection.datetime('createdAt').notNull();
      collection.datetime('updatedAt').notNull();

      collection.primary('id', { name: 'pk_devices' });
      collection.unique('code', { name: 'uq_devices_code' });
      collection.index('status', { name: 'ix_devices_status' });
    });
    上游 Issue 草稿 · Markdown
    ### [重要][能力缺口] @nocobase/db 未公开唯一约束冲突识别，Repository 普通写入泄漏驱动错误

**相关对象**：`@nocobase/db`（评审时 1.0.0-beta.21）、`@nocobase/repository-input`（评审时 0.1.0-beta.3）、`packages/@nocobase/db/skills/nocobase-db/SKILL.md`

**反馈证据核对**：候选反馈：证据不足

定向复核未完成：Pi invocation timed out after 180 seconds.


反馈复核是对原始反馈的证据核对，不等于人工确认、上游修复状态或自动修复授权；保留原评审置信度、分类与处理状态。

db 包内部已有健壮的 isUniqueConstraintViolation，覆盖 Oracle -6602、PostgreSQL 23505、MySQL ER_DUP_ENTRY 和 SQLite 错误码，并沿 cause/originalError 链查找；但 db/dist/src/index.d.ts 与 index.js 没有导出该函数。Repository 的 createOne/update 路径不把唯一冲突归一化为 RepositoryError；repository-input 的错误目录只有 FIELD_NOT_FOUND、RECORD_NOT_FOUND、SCOPE_VIOLATION 等，没有 UNIQUE_CONFLICT/DUPLICATE。设备应用只能复制驱动码判断，并在 service 中先查后写。

**影响**：应用为一条业务唯一约束就要维护跨数据库驱动码副本；预检查在并发创建下存在竞态窗口，两个同 code 请求仍可能同时通过预检查，最终一个请求收到未归一化的驱动错误而不是稳定的 409/ALREADY_EXISTS。

**建议改动**：从 @nocobase/db 公开 isUniqueConstraintViolation 或更高层的 normalizeRepositoryError；在 repository-input 增加 UNIQUE_CONFLICT（映射 ALREADY_EXISTS/409），让 Repository createOne/update 在写路径统一转换唯一冲突；在 db Skill 增加“业务唯一约束 → 统一冲突错误”的 recipe。

**验收标准**：两个并发请求创建同一 code 时，一个成功，另一个稳定返回 409 和 ALREADY_EXISTS 或公开的 UNIQUE_CONFLICT reason；应用无需导入或复制任何数据库驱动错误码。

**评审时证据（冻结快照）**：
- `packages/@nocobase/db/dist/src/database/internal/unique-constraint.js:1–24` — 内部唯一约束分类器覆盖 Oracle、PostgreSQL、MySQL、SQLite 错误码，并沿 cause/originalError 链查找且防循环。
- `packages/@nocobase/db/dist/src/index.js:1–41` — db 公开 createDatabaseManager、defineDatabase、RepositoryError、repositoryErrorStatuses 等入口，但没有导出 isUniqueConstraintViolation。
- `packages/@nocobase/db/dist/src/repository/internal/knex-execution-adapter.js:540–562` — upsert 路径使用唯一约束分类器识别冲突，并在冲突后重新查询现有行，避免重复插入。
- `packages/@nocobase/repository-input/dist/errors.js:8–63` — repository-input 错误目录包含 FIELD_NOT_FOUND、RECORD_NOT_FOUND、SCOPE_VIOLATION，但没有唯一冲突或重复记录类别。
- `packages/@nocobase/db/skills/nocobase-db/SKILL.md:137–149` — db Skill 推荐使用 Repository 进行查询，并给出过滤条件入口。
- `packages/@nocobase/db/skills/nocobase-db/SKILL.md:246–258` — db Skill 说明迁移和 upsert 的使用方式，但没有把唯一冲突转业务错误的公开 recipe。
- `app/server/providers/devices.ts:84–119` — 应用复制跨数据库唯一约束判断，覆盖 PostgreSQL、MySQL、SQLite 等驱动码，说明公开入口缺失导致业务侧重复实现。

- `app/database/main/migrations/202610200001_create_devices.ts:14–25` — 迁移为 devices.code 建立 uq_devices_code 唯一索引，数据库层确实拒绝重复 code。

      packages/@nocobase/db/skills/nocobase-db/SKILL.md:137–149E29

      db Skill 推荐使用 Repository 进行查询，并给出过滤条件入口。

      The rules are absolute, because these files are history that has already run on other machines:

- **Self-contained.** Spell out every table, field, index, constraint and metadata operation in the file. Never import a Collection definition, model, registry or shared constant that keeps evolving — that silently changes both the behavior and the checksum of something already applied.
- **Immutable once merged.** Check with `git log -- <file>` if unsure. Before the branch is merged you may correct the file in place; after it, every change is a new, later file. Never hard-code a previous checksum to make an edited migration look untouched. Correcting it in place changes nothing on its own — it is recorded as executed, so a plain apply skips it and the database keeps the schema the old file produced. `pnpm nocobase db redo` rolls the latest batch back and applies it again.
- **The file name body matches `name`**, and the name is unique across every source in the application.
- **Declare `irreversible: true`** when there is no real reverse operation, instead of writing a `down()` that quietly does nothing.
- **A seed is idempotent**, keyed on a stable business value backed by a unique constraint, so a repeated run is a no-op — `upsertOne` is the direct way to express that. A seed context has no `builder` at all, and a seed has no rollback or truncate behavior.

A backfill belongs in the migration that makes it necessary, using `query` from the same context — not in a seed.

Sample data goes in the same `seeds` directory, declared with `defineSeed({ name, sample: true, run })`. It runs only when the application installs its database with `app.sampleData` set (`APP_SAMPLE_DATA=true`): the connection held no migration or seed history before that start, or `db reset` rebuilt it. Otherwise the seed is recorded as skipped and never runs on its own; `pnpm nocobase db sample` runs every skipped sample seed in development, and a deployment refuses it. Sample data that has to go through other plugins' services, such as projects with issues, is registered instead on `sampleDataToken` from `@nocobase/app-server/sample-data`, from a service provider's `boot()`: `container.resolve(sampleDataToken).register({ name, packageName, run })`. It runs once the application is ready, under the same condition, and is recorded in the default connection's seed history as `sample-data:<name>`. The seed history's `status` column, `executed` or `skipped`, records which happened; the library adds it to an existing history table on the next run.

Both run inside a transaction by default. `transaction: false` opts out, and a failure then leaves partial work behind with no history record, so take it only when the operation genuinely cannot run in one.
      packages/@nocobase/db/skills/nocobase-db/SKILL.md:246–258E30

      db Skill 说明迁移和 upsert 的使用方式，但没有把唯一冲突转业务错误的公开 recipe。

      Resolve the manager once — in a route factory, or as a constructor dependency of a service — not inside every handler. `db.repository()`, `db.query()`, `db.builder()`, `db.collections()` and `db.transaction()` take the connection name as their last argument and use the default connection when it is omitted, so single-connection code never names one.

`filter`, `values`, `select` and `sort` each accept a builder callback or the equivalent serializable JSON AST, and mean the same thing either way. They do not accept a shorthand string array such as `['id', 'name']` or `['-createdAt']`. Field names are Collection logical names, never column names. A write returns a result object — the record is `result.record`, alongside `result.version` and the created relation targets — rather than the record itself. `findMany` is awaited for an array or iterated asynchronously to consume a large result incrementally.

The methods are `findOne`, `findMany`, `count`, `exists`, `aggregate`, `groupBy`, `createOne`, `createMany`, `updateOne`, `updateMany`, `upsertOne`, `deleteOne` and `deleteMany`; `describeMutation` and `validateMutation` report capabilities and pre-validate input, and `withPolicy` returns a scoped Repository.

Boundaries that are easy to get wrong:

- `findOne` takes the first match; `updateOne` and `deleteOne` require exactly one; `upsertOne` requires a unique equality condition.
- `updateMany` and `deleteMany` refuse an empty scope. Pass `all: true` only when the task explicitly calls for every row — never to make an error go away.
- `createMany` and `updateMany` do not accept nested relation writes. `createOne` accepts `create` and `connect` on a relation, not the full set.
- `disconnect` detaches; `delete` removes the target record. A relation target update or delete stays inside the current parent record's scope.
- Repository does not apply the application's permissions. A route handler decides what the caller may see and change _before_ anything reaches `filter`, `values` or `select`. `writePolicy` defaults to `true` for an internal Repository and `false` for one reached from an API route, so a route-facing Repository needs an explicit allow list.
      packages/@nocobase/db/README.zh-CN.md:98–102E31

      README 声称 Repository、Select、Filter 仍是未来提案，与当前导出和 Skill 推荐不一致。

      ## 当前边界

- Repository、Select AST、Filter Builder、Filter AST 和 Sort AST 是未来提案，当前不可调用。
- QueryAdapter 是数据库层查询接口，不读取 Collection Metadata。
- `connection.client()` 是底层 adapter 逃生口，不是常规数据库入口。
      app/server/providers/devices.ts:151–175E33

      应用在 create/update 中先查询 code 是否已存在，再写入并在 catch 中兜底转换冲突。

          async create(input) {
      if (await codeIsTaken(input.code)) {
        throw new DeviceCodeTakenError();
      }

      const now = new Date();
      try {
        const result = await devices.createOne({
          values: {
            id: randomUUID(),
            code: input.code,
            name: input.name,
            status: input.status,
            createdAt: now,
            updatedAt: now,
          },
        });
        return toView(result.record);
      } catch (error) {
        if (isUniqueConstraintViolation(error)) {
          throw new DeviceCodeTakenError();
        }
        throw error;
      }
    },

      F2 · AppI18nError 进入统一 API 错误体需要每个路由自写适配器

      NocoBase3 内核 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-i18n/SKILL.md · @nocobase/app-server · @nocobase/i18n · 原评审：未解决

      ⌄

      反馈证据核对

      定向复核未完成：Pi invocation timed out after 180 seconds.

        反馈复核是对原始反馈的证据核对，不等于人工确认、上游修复状态或自动修复授权；保留原评审置信度、分类与处理状态。

        AppI18nError 暴露 code、ns、key、params、status，但没有 ApiError 的 reason。app-server 的 recognizeApiError 不识别该类型，通用 apiErrorHandler 最终走 readStatusError，只保留 HTTP 状态并把消息呈现为 CODE: ns:key；localizedMessage 不会自动进入响应。i18n Skill 因而要求在路由 onError 中调用 toXApiError 和 serializeI18nError。设备应用为避开这层适配，改成自定义 DeviceCodeTakenError 并在路由映射 reason。

        实际影响
        每个使用 AppI18nError 的路由都要重复安装适配器；漏装时客户端拿到 HTTP_404 类 reason 和原始 CODE 文本，无法稳定本地化，排查时也容易误判成框架未本地化。
        建议改法
        在 app-server 的错误识别链中识别 AppI18nError，或提供官方 i18nErrorToApiError 适配器并在 apiErrorHandler 默认调用；保留可覆盖的 serialize 扩展点，同时把 Skill 的 per-route 示例改成可选的高级用法。
        诊断 · 触发条件、应有行为、绕行代价与验收标准
        触发条件
        路由直接抛 AppI18nError，且只安装通用 apiErrorHandler，没有按路由编写 toXApiError/serializeI18nError。
        应有能力 / 行为
        统一错误处理器识别 AppI18nError，输出稳定的 reason、状态码和 localizedMessage，客户端无需区分错误来源。
        实际观察
        通用处理器只按 status 走 readStatusError，reason 变成 HTTP_404 类值，message 为 CODE: ns:key，AppI18nError 的结构化字段丢失。
        应用绕行与代价
        按 i18n Skill 在每个路由的 onError 中手工转换并序列化；设备应用则完全绕开 AppI18nError，改用自定义异常和稳定 reason。
        改进后的验收标准
        路由抛出带 ns/key/params/status 的 AppI18nError 时，仅安装通用 /api 错误处理器即可返回对应状态、稳定 reason 和 localizedMessage；客户端按 reason 翻译无需路由特判。
        评审时证据 · 冻结快照 6 条
        app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-i18n/SKILL.md:180–212E10

        Skill 明确 /api 失败必须是标准错误体，并给出 AppI18nError 到 ApiError 的转换要求。

        ## Translating on the server

Server text lives in the package's `server/locales/`, wired as above, and is translated in the language of whoever it is for.

Inside a request, the i18n middleware has already resolved the language (session choice, then `Accept-Language`, then the default) and loaded it:

```ts
import { getRequestTranslator } from '@nocobase/i18n/server';

const NS = '@acme/app-plugin-orders';

router.get('/orders/:id', async (context) => {
  const t = getRequestTranslator(context, NS);
  return context.json({
    data: { message: t('orders.archived', { id: context.req.param('id') }) },
  });
});
```

For an error the caller should see in its own language, throw an `AppI18nError` instead of translating a message. It carries a stable code, the namespace, the key and the parameters, and is translated only when it is serialized:

```ts
import { AppI18nError } from '@nocobase/i18n/server';

throw new AppI18nError('ORDER_NOT_FOUND', {
  status: 404,
  ns: NS,
  key: 'errors.notFound',
  params: { id },
});
```

Under `/api` every failure is the standard error body, so an `AppI18nError` that reaches the application on its own is rendered only by its status: its namespace, key, parameters and translated text are lost. The router's `onError` translates it into an `ApiError` and hands everything else on unchanged. The error's code becomes `reason`, the translated text `localizedMessage`, and `ns`, `key` and `params` go in `metadata` so the browser can re-render it in the language its interface is showing:
        app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-i18n/SKILL.md:214–255E11

        Skill 给出 toXApiError 与 serializeI18nError 的路由 onError 适配示例及 localizedMessage 处理。

        ```ts
import { i18nToken } from '@nocobase/app-server/i18n';
import {
  ApiError,
  apiErrorHandler,
  apiErrorStatusFromHttp,
} from '@nocobase/app-server/router';
import {
  type AppI18nError,
  getRequestLocale,
  isAppI18nError,
  serializeI18nError,
} from '@nocobase/i18n/server';
import type { Context } from 'hono';

const i18n = container.resolve(i18nToken);

function toOrdersApiError(error: AppI18nError, context: Context): ApiError {
  const locale = getRequestLocale(context) ?? 'en-US';
  const serialized = serializeI18nError(i18n, error, locale);
  return new ApiError({
    status: apiErrorStatusFromHttp(error.status),
    reason: serialized.code,
    domain: 'orders',
    message: error.message,
    localizedMessage: { locale, message: serialized.message },
    metadata: {
      ns: serialized.ns,
      key: serialized.key,
      ...(serialized.params ? { params: serialized.params } : {}),
    },
    cause: error,
  });
}

router.onError((error, context) =>
  apiErrorHandler(
    isAppI18nError(error) ? toOrdersApiError(error, context) : error,
    context,
  ),
);
```
        packages/@nocobase/app-server/dist/router/api-error.js:89–127E22

        recognizeApiError 依次识别 ApiError、HTTPException、RepositoryError 和带 4xx 状态的错误。

        /**
 * The `ApiError` for an error the framework recognizes, or `undefined` for one it does not:
 *
 * - an `ApiError` as it is;
 * - Hono's `HTTPException`, and any error that carries a 4xx `status` the way Hono's `getResponse()` convention does
 *   (such as `AuthorizationDeniedError`), with their status and message, and a string `reason` and `domain` on such an
 *   error kept too;
 * - a `RepositoryError` whose `status` is not `INTERNAL` (a refused write, a missing record, a version conflict), with
 *   that status, its code as `reason`, domain `app`, and its `path` and `details` as `metadata`. One that is the
 *   server's own fault, such as an invalid Policy, is not recognized.
 */
export function recognizeApiError(error) {
    if (error instanceof ApiError)
        return error;
    if (error instanceof HTTPException) {
        return new ApiError({
            status: apiErrorStatusFromHttp(error.status),
            reason: `HTTP_${error.status}`,
            domain: appErrorDomain,
            message: error.message || `Request failed with status ${error.status}.`,
            httpStatus: error.status,
            cause: error,
        });
    }
    const repositoryError = repositoryApiError(error);
    if (repositoryError)
        return repositoryError;
    const statusError = readStatusError(error);
    if (statusError) {
        return new ApiError({
            status: apiErrorStatusFromHttp(statusError.status),
            reason: statusError.reason ?? `HTTP_${statusError.status}`,
            domain: statusError.domain ?? appErrorDomain,
            message: statusError.message,
            httpStatus: statusError.status,
            cause: error,
        });
    }
    return undefined;
        packages/@nocobase/app-server/dist/router/api-error.js:143–178E23

        readStatusError 只按 status 生成 HTTP_ 前缀 reason，未保留 AppI18nError 的 ns/key/params。

        function readStatusError(error) {
    if (!(error instanceof Error) || !('status' in error))
        return undefined;
    const { status } = error;
    if (typeof status !== 'number' || status < 400 || status > 499)
        return undefined;
    const fields = error;
    return {
        status,
        message: error.message,
        ...(typeof fields.reason === 'string' ? { reason: fields.reason } : {}),
        ...(typeof fields.domain === 'string' ? { domain: fields.domain } : {}),
    };
}
export function apiErrorBody(error, requestId) {
    return { error: error.toPayload(requestId) };
}
/**
 * Answer with the standard error body for `error`, carrying the request's id; anything unrecognized is an opaque 500.
 * Framework-internal: plugins answer through `apiErrorHandler`, which rethrows what it does not recognize.
 */
function apiErrorResponse(context, error) {
    const apiError = toApiError(error);
    return context.json(apiErrorBody(apiError, getRequestId(context)), apiError.code);
}
/**
 * The `onError` for a plugin's router: it answers every error `recognizeApiError` recognizes in the standard body and
 * rethrows the rest, so an enclosing router can still translate it and the application answers an unexpected failure
 * with an opaque 500. A router that translates its own domain errors does that first and then delegates here. Install
 * it even though the application renders the same errors: a router tested on a bare Hono has no `/api` handler.
 */
export function apiErrorHandler(error, context) {
    const known = recognizeApiError(error);
    if (known)
        return apiErrorResponse(context, known);
    throw error;
        packages/@nocobase/i18n/dist/server/errors.js:1–42E24

        AppI18nError 保存 code、ns、key、params、status，并可由 serializeI18nError 生成带 localizedMessage 的载荷。

        /**
 * An error that carries what it needs to be translated, rather than a message translated when it was thrown.
 *
 * Translation happens at serialization, where the request's locale is known. The same error therefore renders in
 * whatever language the caller asked for, and constructing one never requires knowing the locale.
 */
export class AppI18nError extends Error {
    code;
    ns;
    key;
    params;
    status;
    constructor(code, options) {
        // The untranslated message is what logs and stack traces show; the translated one is produced on serialization.
        super(`${code}: ${options.ns}:${options.key}`, { cause: options.cause });
        this.name = 'AppI18nError';
        this.code = code;
        this.ns = options.ns;
        this.key = options.key;
        this.params = options.params;
        this.status = options.status ?? 400;
    }
}
export function isAppI18nError(error) {
    return error instanceof AppI18nError;
}
/**
 * Renders an error for a response body.
 *
 * `message` is translated into the request's locale so an API-only application needs nothing else, while `ns`, `key`,
 * and `params` let a frontend re-render it in whatever language its interface is currently showing.
 */
export function serializeI18nError(runtime, error, locale) {
    const translate = runtime.getFixedT(error.ns, locale);
    return {
        code: error.code,
        message: translate(error.key, error.params),
        ns: error.ns,
        key: error.key,
        ...(error.params ? { params: error.params } : {}),
    };
}
        app/server/routes/devices.ts:31–56E35

        应用把 DeviceCodeTakenError/DeviceNotFoundError 映射为 ALREADY_EXISTS/DEVICE_CODE_TAKEN 与 NOT_FOUND/DEVICE_NOT_FOUND，绕开 AppI18nError。

        /** Answers an expected business failure without passing it through the application's 500 logger. */
function respondToDeviceError(error: unknown, context: Context): Response {
  const mapped = deviceError(error);
  if (!(mapped instanceof ApiError)) throw error;
  return apiErrorHandler(mapped, context);
}

function deviceError(error: unknown): unknown {
  if (error instanceof DeviceCodeTakenError) {
    return new ApiError({
      status: 'ALREADY_EXISTS',
      reason: 'DEVICE_CODE_TAKEN',
      domain: DOMAIN,
      message: 'A device with this code already exists.',
    });
  }
  if (error instanceof DeviceNotFoundError) {
    return new ApiError({
      status: 'NOT_FOUND',
      reason: 'DEVICE_NOT_FOUND',
      domain: DOMAIN,
      message: 'The requested device does not exist.',
    });
  }
  return error;
}
        上游 Issue 草稿 · Markdown
        ### [轻微][易用性改进] AppI18nError 进入统一 API 错误体需要每个路由自写适配器

**相关对象**：`app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-i18n/SKILL.md`、`@nocobase/app-server`（评审时 2.0.0-beta.8）、`@nocobase/i18n`（评审时 1.0.0-beta.7）

**反馈证据核对**：候选反馈：证据不足

定向复核未完成：Pi invocation timed out after 180 seconds.


反馈复核是对原始反馈的证据核对，不等于人工确认、上游修复状态或自动修复授权；保留原评审置信度、分类与处理状态。

AppI18nError 暴露 code、ns、key、params、status，但没有 ApiError 的 reason。app-server 的 recognizeApiError 不识别该类型，通用 apiErrorHandler 最终走 readStatusError，只保留 HTTP 状态并把消息呈现为 CODE: ns:key；localizedMessage 不会自动进入响应。i18n Skill 因而要求在路由 onError 中调用 toXApiError 和 serializeI18nError。设备应用为避开这层适配，改成自定义 DeviceCodeTakenError 并在路由映射 reason。

**影响**：每个使用 AppI18nError 的路由都要重复安装适配器；漏装时客户端拿到 HTTP_404 类 reason 和原始 CODE 文本，无法稳定本地化，排查时也容易误判成框架未本地化。

**建议改动**：在 app-server 的错误识别链中识别 AppI18nError，或提供官方 i18nErrorToApiError 适配器并在 apiErrorHandler 默认调用；保留可覆盖的 serialize 扩展点，同时把 Skill 的 per-route 示例改成可选的高级用法。

**验收标准**：路由抛出带 ns/key/params/status 的 AppI18nError 时，仅安装通用 /api 错误处理器即可返回对应状态、稳定 reason 和 localizedMessage；客户端按 reason 翻译无需路由特判。

**评审时证据（冻结快照）**：
- `app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-i18n/SKILL.md:180–212` — Skill 明确 /api 失败必须是标准错误体，并给出 AppI18nError 到 ApiError 的转换要求。
- `app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-i18n/SKILL.md:214–255` — Skill 给出 toXApiError 与 serializeI18nError 的路由 onError 适配示例及 localizedMessage 处理。
- `packages/@nocobase/app-server/dist/router/api-error.js:89–127` — recognizeApiError 依次识别 ApiError、HTTPException、RepositoryError 和带 4xx 状态的错误。
- `packages/@nocobase/app-server/dist/router/api-error.js:143–178` — readStatusError 只按 status 生成 HTTP_ 前缀 reason，未保留 AppI18nError 的 ns/key/params。
- `packages/@nocobase/i18n/dist/server/errors.js:1–42` — AppI18nError 保存 code、ns、key、params、status，并可由 serializeI18nError 生成带 localizedMessage 的载荷。
- `app/server/routes/devices.ts:31–56` — 应用把 DeviceCodeTakenError/DeviceNotFoundError 映射为 ALREADY_EXISTS/DEVICE_CODE_TAKEN 与 NOT_FOUND/DEVICE_NOT_FOUND，绕开 AppI18nError。

        实现者补充建议

        来自可选过程笔记，不代表已独立确认；相同建议引用上方评审，其余保留原文。

        建议 01 · frontend

        Base UI Select needs `items` to localize its trigger

        可自动化

        The template's shadcn Select is built on Base UI, whose Select.Value renders the raw value unless the root receives an `items` list of { value, label }. The devices page passed localized SelectItem children but no `items`, so the popup was translated while the closed trigger showed 'available' / 'all'. Other pages in the app (inbox, permission editor, user form, settings users) already pass `items`, which is what made this page's omission easy to miss.

        建议改动

        Add a lint or review rule (or a shared wrapper) that requires a Select's trigger to be backed by `items` whenever its values are enum codes, and cover each business page's closed select text with a component test rather than only asserting the options in the open popup.

        来源：Agent · retro.json

        框架负责运行时、命名空间隔离、资源查找与回退、React 接入和测试 Provider；应用负责业务文案 key 与页面组件选择。
        推荐使用方式
        插件通过 defineI18n 注册应用命名空间和资源，页面使用 useTranslation；服务端复用同一命名空间；测试用 TestI18nProvider 注入固定语言。

        应用使用 useTranslation、t 和 route title 文案；B01 首轮状态选择框显示 all/available/borrowed 原始值，根因是应用 Base UI Select 未提供 items 列表，focused 复测已通过，表格状态翻译正常。

        Agent / 业务实现
        E1 E2 E4 E6 E7 E36 E38 E39
        缺少某个命名空间或语言资源时仍能显示可理解的文本，加载失败后可恢复。直接支持
        框架职责与业务边界
        框架负责 fallback 链、并发加载去重、失败不缓存和 Provider 生命周期；应用负责提供默认语言资源。
        推荐使用方式
        使用运行时默认 fallback（当前语言 → 应用默认语言 → en-US），并在应用启动前预加载当前语言。
        Agent 实际使用
        运行时按当前语言、应用默认语言、en-US 顺序查找；加载失败的 Promise 不写入缓存，后续调用可重试；应用客户端在渲染前预加载全部贡献资源。

        useTranslation/NamespaceScope 与测试 Provider 的入口直接，Provider 可选运行时便于宿主复用；复杂插件的命名空间仍需理解应用命名空间与插件命名空间两级关系，但 Skill 给出了单一接入规则。

        E4 E5 E7 E8

        Agent 友好度

        90/100

        Skill 明确要求通过 defineI18n 注册、服务端复用命名空间并在测试中替换 Provider，示例包含 key、参数和错误序列化；Agent 能据此找到入口，B01 的失误属于应用组件层而非指引缺失。

        E6 E7 E8 E9

        设计合理性

        91/100

        一个 Provider 持有运行时，NamespaceScope 在渲染树中限定命名空间，资源覆盖按注册顺序合并，测试与生产走同一运行时抽象；边界清晰且没有把翻译逻辑散落到页面。

        E1 E4 E5

        实现完整性与可靠性

        89/100

        语言加载在初始化前完成，失败加载不缓存，回退链覆盖当前语言缺失和单 key 缺失，翻译器在资源变化后重新解析；这些实现兑现了公开回退约定。

        E2 E3 E4

        语言切换边界 · useAppLocale 使用方式

        E19

        需求 → 框架职责 → 推荐与实际接入

        切换语言后当前页面立即更新，刷新后保持，并在重新登录或另一独立会话中保持所选语言。直接支持
        框架职责与业务边界
        框架负责运行时切换、浏览器持久化、服务端偏好同步和启动时预加载；应用只负责渲染选择器。
        推荐使用方式
        使用 useAppLocale() 读取和更新语言；Provider 负责初始化运行时并从服务端会话恢复偏好，应用不要自行写 localStorage 或改运行时。
        Agent 实际使用
        应用语言切换器调用 useAppLocale()；hook 先等待资源加载，再设置运行时、写入 nocobase.locale，并 PUT i18n/locale 通知服务端；独立会话 B06 通过。
        缺口归因
        未记录能力缺口
        E12 E13 E16 E17 E19 E36 E38
        切换失败或服务端同步失败时不能留下半切换状态或错误语言。直接支持
        框架职责与业务边界
        框架负责资源加载失败、存储不可用和同步失败的边界；应用负责提示用户。
        推荐使用方式
        依赖 useAppLocale 的原子切换语义；同步失败时保留已切换的浏览器语言，不强制回滚。
        Agent 实际使用
        实现先 await 语言资源加载，再设置 locale 和存储；同步请求失败只记录错误，不回滚运行时或本地存储；存储访问有 try/catch 和 SSR 保护。
        缺口归因
        未记录能力缺口
        E12 E13 E14 E15 E17

        需求满足度

        89/100

        useAppLocale 把运行时切换、localStorage 和服务端 PUT 串成一个入口，应用只需渲染选择器；独立会话验证了持久化路径，满足本次中英切换与保持需求。

        E12 E13 E14 E16 E17

        使用便利度

        86/100

        hook 直接暴露 locale、setLocale、availableLocales 和加载状态，调用方无需了解存储 key 或服务端端点；唯一需要注意的是同一浏览器多标签共享存储，README 已明示。

        E12 E14 E16

        Agent 友好度

        87/100

        Skill 明确“不要自建选择器，使用 useAppLocale()”，README 给出持久化 key、默认语言和服务端同步端点；Agent 按此接入即可，无需猜测状态流。

        E12 E14 E15 E19

        设计合理性

        87/100

        浏览器存储、应用运行时和会话同步分层清楚，Provider 负责初始化，hook 只组合动作；同步失败不回滚 UI，避免网络错误导致用户语言跳变。

        E12 E13 E17

        实现完整性与可靠性

        85/100

        切换前等待资源加载，写入存储对不可用环境有保护，服务端同步失败不会破坏当前运行时；同一账号多标签共享 localStorage 的覆盖风险是已知限制，不影响独立会话路径。

        E12 E13 E14 E17

        覆盖限制：同一账号多标签共写 localStorage 的限制只来自 README 说明，未做多标签实测；没有审阅语言切换的服务端存储实现。

        AppI18nError 转 ApiError · 路由 onError 适配

        E10 E11

        需求 → 框架职责 → 推荐与实际接入

        服务端错误返回稳定状态码、reason 和字段错误，客户端能按 reason 显示本地化业务消息。正常组合
        框架职责与业务边界
        框架负责统一错误体、状态映射和识别顺序；应用负责业务 reason、域和本地化文案。
        推荐使用方式
        业务抛 ApiError 或经路由适配器转 ApiError；客户端按 reason 映射文案，localizedMessage 作为可选服务端翻译。
        Agent 实际使用
        应用定义 DeviceCodeTakenError/DeviceNotFoundError，在路由中映射为 ALREADY_EXISTS/DEVICE_CODE_TAKEN 和 NOT_FOUND/DEVICE_NOT_FOUND，客户端按 reason 翻译；未使用 AppI18nError。
        缺口归因
        未记录能力缺口
        E20 E21 E22 E35
        服务端抛出的 AppI18nError 能直接进入统一错误体，保留本地化消息和稳定 reason。需要绕行
        框架职责与业务边界
        框架负责提供从 i18n 错误到 API 错误的通用适配；应用负责在各路由安装适配器。
        推荐使用方式
        Skill 要求在路由 onError 中调用 toXApiError 和 serializeI18nError，把 AppI18nError 转成 ApiError。
        Agent 实际使用
        通用 apiErrorHandler 不识别 AppI18nError，只按 status 走 readStatusError，产生 HTTP_404 类 reason 和 CODE: ns:key 原始文本；应用因此绕开 AppI18nError，改用自定义 reason。
        缺口归因
        NocoBase3 内核
        E10 E11 E22 E23 E24 E35

        需求满足度

        86/100

        ApiError 的状态码、reason、domain、fieldViolations 和 localizedMessage 能覆盖设备台账的服务端错误；应用用稳定 reason 加客户端翻译完成了需求，但 AppI18nError 需要额外适配器。

        E20 E21 E22 E10

        使用便利度

        80/100

        构造 ApiError 和挂 apiErrorHandler 很直接，状态码枚举也有限；但抛 AppI18nError 时通用处理器不会自动保留本地化字段，调用方必须知道按路由安装 onError 适配器。

        E22 E23 E24 E11

        Agent 友好度

        88/100

        Skill 明确写出 AppI18nError 与 /api 错误体的边界，并给出 toXApiError、serializeI18nError 的完整片段；应用选择 stable reason 也有指引依据，因此 Agent 不易误判为运行时缺陷。

        E10 E11 E20

        设计合理性

        82/100

        统一错误体和识别顺序边界清楚，业务 reason 与 HTTP 状态解耦；不足是 i18n 错误到 API 错误的转换被推给每个路由，形成重复适配层，且通用 fallback 会丢掉 i18n 的结构化字段。

        E20 E21 E22 E23 E24

        实现完整性与可靠性

        83/100

        recognizeApiError 对 ApiError、HTTPException、RepositoryError 和内部分支的顺序可预测，fieldViolations 与状态映射稳定；AppI18nError 走 fallback 时只保留状态码和原始 CODE 文本，可靠性依赖调用方是否安装适配器。

        E22 E23 E24

        覆盖限制：未审阅应用所有路由的异常路径；AppI18nError 的跨包依赖方向和服务端路由注册器全部实现未覆盖。

        映射验收项：B02, B03, B04

        Repository 查询 · upsert 与迁移 · 唯一索引

        E29 E30

        需求 → 框架职责 → 推荐与实际接入

        设备 code 在创建和编辑时必须被唯一约束拒绝，并向客户端返回可本地化的冲突错误。需要绕行
        框架职责与业务边界
        框架负责唯一索引、冲突识别和稳定的公开错误入口；应用负责把冲突映射为设备业务消息。
        推荐使用方式
        迁移中用 collection.unique 建唯一索引，Repository 写入依赖数据库约束；若框架公开唯一冲突错误，应用直接映射为 ALREADY_EXISTS。
        Agent 实际使用
        应用迁移建立 uq_devices_code，但 @nocobase/db 未公开 isUniqueConstraintViolation，Repository 也不把驱动唯一错误转成统一错误；应用复制 30 余行驱动码判断，并在 service 中先查后写、再 catch 兜底。
        缺口归因
        NocoBase3 内核
        E25 E26 E27 E28 E32 E33 E34
        按当前指引能发现 Repository、迁移和 upsert 的正确用法，避免退回手写 SQL。直接支持
        框架职责与业务边界
        框架负责公开 API 与类型；文档负责说明哪些能力已经可用。
        推荐使用方式
        阅读 db Skill 的 Repository/迁移/upsert 章节，使用 Repository 查询和写入，用 upsert 处理已存在记录。
        Agent 实际使用
        db Skill 明确要求优先 Repository 并给出 upsert 示例，应用也使用了 Repository；但 README.zh-CN.md 仍把 Repository/Select/Filter 写成未来提案，与 index 导出和实际使用矛盾。
        缺口归因
        文档 / Skill / 示例
        E26 E29 E30 E31 E33

        需求满足度

        72/100

        唯一索引、Repository 和 upsert 都存在，迁移也能声明 uq_devices_code；但重复 code 的冲突识别没有公开入口，应用必须复制驱动错误判断并加预检查，属于按公开 API 使用仍要绕行的能力缺口。

        E25 E26 E27 E28 E34

        使用便利度

        68/100

        Repository 的查询、写入和 upsert 入口清楚，迁移 DSL 也直接；冲突场景却要理解各数据库驱动码并复制内部 helper，应用为一条业务唯一约束增加了额外服务和预检查代码。

        E26 E27 E28 E32 E33

        Agent 友好度

        64/100

        db Skill 对 Repository、迁移和 upsert 的说明足以让 Agent 找到主路径，但没有“唯一冲突如何转业务错误”的公开 recipe；同时 README 把已存在能力写成未来提案，容易让 Agent 放弃 Repository。

        E29 E30 E31

        设计合理性

        73/100

        内部唯一约束分类器覆盖 Oracle、PostgreSQL、MySQL、SQLite 并沿 cause 链查找，设计本身稳健；问题是它没有成为公开抽象，repository-input 错误目录也缺少 UNIQUE/DUPLICATE 类别，导致边界断裂。

        E25 E27 E28

        实现完整性与可靠性

        76/100

        upsert 路径先用分类器判断唯一冲突并重新查询，能避免并发下的重复插入；create/update 路径直接把驱动错误抛给上层，应用预检查存在竞态窗口，因此普通写入的冲突可靠性低于 upsert。

        E25 E27 E32 E33

        覆盖限制：快照缺少 packages/@nocobase/db/docs 目录，无法核对 README 所链接文档；未执行并发写入；未覆盖所有数据库驱动的真实错误形态。

        F4 · i18n 命名空间、回退和测试装配形成一致闭环

        NocoBase3 内核 · @nocobase/i18n · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-i18n/SKILL.md

        ⌄

        运行时按当前语言 → 应用默认语言 → en-US 回退，单 key 缺失也逐级查找；Provider 在渲染树中限定命名空间并合并应用命名空间；测试 Provider 复用同一运行时，允许注入固定语言和资源。Skill 把 defineI18n 注册、服务端命名空间复用和测试替换写成同一套接入规则。

        提供的帮助
        应用无需自建翻译缓存、fallback 或测试替身；页面、服务端错误和测试共享同一命名空间约定，减少“生产能翻、测试不翻”的偏差。
        应保留的能力
        保持现有运行时与测试 Provider 的同一抽象，不需要为本案例改动。

        E1 · packages/@nocobase/i18n/dist/core/runtime.js:28–43

        运行时初始化 availableLocales、默认语言和资源缓存，建立应用命名空间到语言资源的查找基础。

            /**
     * The languages on offer: whichever the application's own locale files declare, plus the default.
     *
     * A plugin's locale file supplies translations, not languages — an installed plugin that happens to ship `ja-JP`
     * must not put Japanese in the application's picker. The default is always present so a resolution has somewhere to
     * land even before any namespace has registered, and it is appended rather than prepended so changing it does not
     * reorder a language picker.
     */
    computeLocales() {
        const applicationNamespace = this.registry.getApplicationNamespace();
        const declared = this.declaredLocales ??
            (applicationNamespace
                ? this.registry.getNamespaceLocales(applicationNamespace)
                : []);
        return [...new Set([...declared, this.defaultLocale])];
    }

        原文件 SHA-256：c0c01a640d42896dcd87bbd8362649ee1307b9d3e072649a09d021fe98e32469

        E2 · packages/@nocobase/i18n/dist/core/runtime.js:122–157

        语言加载先查缓存，缺失时加载；加载 Promise 失败后从缓存移除，后续可重试。

            /**
     * Initializes the instance for a locale, loading that locale's resources first so the first render is already
     * translated and no loading state is needed.
     */
    async init(locale = this.defaultLocale) {
        if (this.initialized) {
            await this.changeLanguage(locale);
            return this.i18n;
        }
        const resolved = this.resolveLocale(locale);
        this.initialized = true;
        await this.i18n.init({
            lng: resolved,
            // A chain, not one language: the application's default first, then English. A plugin that has not translated
            // the language in use falls back to the application's default, and to English when it lacks that one too — which
            // is what an application defaulting to Chinese and adding Spanish leaves a plugin facing.
            fallbackLng: [...new Set([this.defaultLocale, BASE_LOCALE])],
            defaultNS: this.registry.getApplicationNamespace() ?? BASE_NAMESPACE,
            // Keys nest, so `trigger.types.schedule` addresses a tree. Namespaces never travel inside the key: they are
            // passed through options, which keeps a colon in a key from being read as a namespace separator.
            keySeparator: '.',
            nsSeparator: false,
            // Resources are added by `ensureLocaleLoaded` rather than by a backend plugin, so there is nothing to defer and
            // `t()` is usable the moment `init` resolves.
            initAsync: false,
            interpolation: { escapeValue: false },
            react: {
                // Without this react-i18next keeps only the first namespace of the list it is given, which would drop the
                // fallback chain that `NamespaceScope` passes down and leave a plugin unable to reuse application wording.
                nsMode: 'fallback',
            },
            ...this.options.initOptions,
        });
        await this.ensureLocaleLoaded(resolved);
        return this.i18n;
    }

        原文件 SHA-256：c0c01a640d42896dcd87bbd8362649ee1307b9d3e072649a09d021fe98e32469

        E3 · packages/@nocobase/i18n/dist/core/runtime.js:161–199

        翻译查找按当前语言、应用默认语言、en-US 逐级回退，并在命名空间内解析 key。

            /** Loads the locale's resources across every namespace, then switches, so the change lands everywhere at once. */
    async changeLanguage(locale) {
        const resolved = this.resolveLocale(locale);
        await this.ensureLocaleLoaded(resolved);
        await this.i18n.changeLanguage(resolved);
        return resolved;
    }
    /**
     * A translator bound to a namespace, and optionally to a locale.
     *
     * Pass `locale` explicitly for anything leaving the process — mail, notifications, queue jobs — because the language
     * of outbound content follows its recipient, not whoever triggered the work.
     */
    getFixedT(namespace, locale) {
        // The chain is passed as an ordered namespace list rather than through `fallbackNS`, which i18next only reads from
        // instance options — a single instance serves every namespace here, and each one needs its own chain.
        const chain = this.getNamespaceChain(namespace);
        // i18next types `t` against namespaces known at compile time, while every namespace here is a package name
        // resolved at runtime. This is the one place that gap is bridged, and it is narrowed to the real contract —
        // a key plus options in, a string out — rather than widened away.
        const translate = this.i18n.getFixedT(locale ?? null, chain);
        return (key, options) => {
            // An explicit `ns` overrides the binding, and still falls back through the chain behind it.
            const namespaces = options?.ns
                ? this.getNamespaceChain(options.ns)
                : undefined;
            return translate(key, {
                ...options,
                ...(namespaces ? { ns: namespaces } : {}),
            });
        };
    }
    /** A namespace followed by its fallbacks, with `APP_NS` resolved to the application's package name. */
    getNamespaceChain(namespace) {
        return [
            this.registry.resolveNamespace(namespace),
            ...this.registry.getFallbackNamespaces(namespace),
        ];
    }

        原文件 SHA-256：c0c01a640d42896dcd87bbd8362649ee1307b9d3e072649a09d021fe98e32469

        E4 · packages/@nocobase/i18n/dist/client/context.js:1–49

        I18nProvider 持有运行时，useOptionalI18nRuntime 允许宿主复用，NamespaceScope 为子树限定命名空间。

        import { Fragment, createContext, createElement, useCallback, useContext, useMemo, } from 'react';
import { I18nextProvider, useTranslation as useI18nextTranslation, } from 'react-i18next';
const RuntimeContext = createContext(undefined);
const NamespaceContext = createContext(undefined);
/**
 * Puts the runtime and its i18next instance in context. Mounted once, above everything that translates.
 */
export function I18nProvider({ runtime, children, }) {
    return createElement(RuntimeContext.Provider, { value: runtime }, createElement(I18nextProvider, { i18n: runtime.i18n }, children));
}
export function useI18nRuntime() {
    const runtime = useOptionalI18nRuntime();
    if (!runtime) {
        throw new Error('No i18n runtime in context. Render the tree inside <I18nProvider>.');
    }
    return runtime;
}
/**
 * The runtime if one is mounted, `undefined` otherwise.
 *
 * A component that merely offers translation — a language switcher, say — should render nothing rather than break a
 * tree that has no i18n at all, which is what a focused test usually renders.
 */
export function useOptionalI18nRuntime() {
    return useContext(RuntimeContext);
}
/**
 * Makes a namespace the default for everything rendered inside it, so a plugin's own pages translate without naming it.
 *
 * The application host wraps each contribution in this using the package name it already records, which is what lets a
 * plugin page call `useTranslation()` with no arguments.
 *
 * The scope follows the render tree rather than code ownership: a component a plugin exports for the application to
 * render sits in the application's scope, not its own, and has to name its namespace explicitly.
 */
export function NamespaceScope({ ns, children, }) {
    // Scoping is an enhancement, not a requirement: a tree rendered without i18n — a focused test, or a host that has
    // not mounted it — still renders its children rather than failing.
    const runtime = useOptionalI18nRuntime();
    const resolved = useMemo(() => runtime?.registry.resolveNamespace(ns) ?? ns, [runtime, ns]);
    // The whole chain goes into `defaultNS`, not just the namespace itself: i18next reads `fallbackNS` from instance
    // options only, and one instance serves every namespace here, so the ordered list is what carries the fallback.
    const chain = useMemo(() => runtime
        ? [resolved, ...runtime.registry.getFallbackNamespaces(resolved)]
        : [resolved], [runtime, resolved]);
    if (!runtime)
        return createElement(Fragment, null, children);
    return createElement(NamespaceContext.Provider, { value: resolved }, createElement(I18nextProvider, { i18n: runtime.i18n, defaultNS: chain }, children));
}

        E5 · packages/@nocobase/i18n/dist/testing/provider.js:1–13

        TestI18nProvider 基于同一 Provider 注入测试语言和资源，没有另造翻译实现。

        import { createElement, } from 'react';
import { I18nProvider, NamespaceScope } from '../client/context.js';
/**
 * Mounts a test runtime the way an application does: the same provider, and optionally the same namespace scope.
 *
 * It renders the package's own `I18nProvider` and `NamespaceScope` rather than contexts of its own, so what the
 * component under test reads is exactly what it reads in an application.
 */
export function TestI18nProvider({ runtime, namespace, children, }) {
    return createElement(I18nProvider, { runtime }, namespace
        ? createElement(NamespaceScope, { ns: namespace }, children)
        : children);
}

        E6 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-i18n/SKILL.md:34–40

        Skill 规定插件通过 defineI18n 注册应用命名空间和资源，客户端与服务端复用同一命名空间。

        # The one rule that decides everything

**A namespace is a package name, and the namespace in scope follows the render tree.**

An application's pages get the application's namespace; a plugin's pages get that plugin's, because the host wraps each contribution using the `packageName` it already records. So a component in its own package's routes translates with no namespace at all.

The exception is what most mistakes come from: a component a plugin exports for the application to render is in the _application's_ scope, not its own. It has to name its namespace.

        原文件 SHA-256：48013bdbf9a871bde0c159293d1d5804fe5bc8e7384b70bbd9cc9a3308351f8d

        E7 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-i18n/SKILL.md:44–86

        客户端接入示例展示 useTranslation、参数插值和命名空间使用方式。

        ## Wiring locales into a package for the first time

A package has a `client/locales/` and a `server/locales/` tree, each with an `en-US.ts`, the other languages, and an `index.ts` mapping every language to a dynamic import:

```ts
// client/locales/index.ts, and server/locales/index.ts in the same shape
import type { LocaleLoaders } from '@nocobase/i18n';

const locales: LocaleLoaders = {
  'en-US': () => import('./en-US.js'),
  'zh-CN': () => import('./zh-CN.js'),
};

export default locales;
```

Import that module and pass it as `locales`, the same way on both sides:

```ts
// client/plugin.ts
import locales from './locales/index.js';

export default defineClientPlugin({
  packageName: '@acme/app-plugin-orders',
  locales,
  routes,
});
```

```ts
// server/plugin.ts
import locales from './locales/index.js';

export default defineServerPlugin({
  baseDir,
  packageName: '@acme/app-plugin-orders',
  locales,
});
```

Import it statically. Each language is already a separate dynamic import, so a language is loaded only when something needs it; deferring the map as well gains nothing. `locales: () => import('./locales/index.js')` is still accepted on both sides for code written that way.

Without this step every `t()` in the package renders its key. An application's own `client/runtime.ts` and `server/runtime.ts` pass their locales the same way.

        原文件 SHA-256：48013bdbf9a871bde0c159293d1d5804fe5bc8e7384b70bbd9cc9a3308351f8d

        E8 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-i18n/SKILL.md:88–100

        Skill 说明命名空间回退到应用命名空间，并提醒不要为单条文案复制资源。

        ## Adding a string

1. Find the package that owns the text. Application text goes in `client/locales/`; a plugin's text goes in that plugin's `client/locales/`.
2. Add the key to `en-US.ts`. The structure is written once: the type is derived from the value with `LocaleResource<typeof enUS>`, and other locales are annotated with it, which is what makes a typo a compile error.
3. Add the translation to every other locale file. A missing key falls back rather than breaking, so this can lag, and `typecheck` reports it.
4. Render it with `useTranslation` from `@nocobase/i18n/client`.

```tsx
import { useTranslation } from '@nocobase/i18n/client';

const { t } = useTranslation();
t('actions.save');
```

        原文件 SHA-256：48013bdbf9a871bde0c159293d1d5804fe5bc8e7384b70bbd9cc9a3308351f8d

        E9 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-i18n/SKILL.md:162–178

        测试章节要求使用 TestI18nProvider 注入固定语言，避免依赖浏览器语言环境。

        ## Menu and breadcrumb titles

Routes are declared before any language is known, so a route carries the key, not the text. Write a key from the owning package's locale file in `navigation.title` and `breadcrumb.title`:

```ts
defineAppRoutes([
  {
    name: 'orders',
    path: '/orders',
    navigation: { title: 'nav.orders', icon: ShoppingCart },
    breadcrumb: { title: 'nav.orders' },
    componentLoader: () => import('./pages/orders-page.js'),
  },
]);
```

The navigation and breadcrumbs translate the title in the namespace of the package that declared the route, so no namespace is written. A title with no matching key renders as written, which is how a missing key shows up.

        原文件 SHA-256：48013bdbf9a871bde0c159293d1d5804fe5bc8e7384b70bbd9cc9a3308351f8d

        E10 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-i18n/SKILL.md:180–212

        Skill 明确 /api 失败必须是标准错误体，并给出 AppI18nError 到 ApiError 的转换要求。

        ## Translating on the server

Server text lives in the package's `server/locales/`, wired as above, and is translated in the language of whoever it is for.

Inside a request, the i18n middleware has already resolved the language (session choice, then `Accept-Language`, then the default) and loaded it:

```ts
import { getRequestTranslator } from '@nocobase/i18n/server';

const NS = '@acme/app-plugin-orders';

router.get('/orders/:id', async (context) => {
  const t = getRequestTranslator(context, NS);
  return context.json({
    data: { message: t('orders.archived', { id: context.req.param('id') }) },
  });
});
```

For an error the caller should see in its own language, throw an `AppI18nError` instead of translating a message. It carries a stable code, the namespace, the key and the parameters, and is translated only when it is serialized:

```ts
import { AppI18nError } from '@nocobase/i18n/server';

throw new AppI18nError('ORDER_NOT_FOUND', {
  status: 404,
  ns: NS,
  key: 'errors.notFound',
  params: { id },
});
```

Under `/api` every failure is the standard error body, so an `AppI18nError` that reaches the application on its own is rendered only by its status: its namespace, key, parameters and translated text are lost. The router's `onError` translates it into an `ApiError` and hands everything else on unchanged. The error's code becomes `reason`, the translated text `localizedMessage`, and `ns`, `key` and `params` go in `metadata` so the browser can re-render it in the language its interface is showing:

        原文件 SHA-256：48013bdbf9a871bde0c159293d1d5804fe5bc8e7384b70bbd9cc9a3308351f8d

        E11 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-i18n/SKILL.md:214–255

        Skill 给出 toXApiError 与 serializeI18nError 的路由 onError 适配示例及 localizedMessage 处理。

        ```ts
import { i18nToken } from '@nocobase/app-server/i18n';
import {
  ApiError,
  apiErrorHandler,
  apiErrorStatusFromHttp,
} from '@nocobase/app-server/router';
import {
  type AppI18nError,
  getRequestLocale,
  isAppI18nError,
  serializeI18nError,
} from '@nocobase/i18n/server';
import type { Context } from 'hono';

const i18n = container.resolve(i18nToken);

function toOrdersApiError(error: AppI18nError, context: Context): ApiError {
  const locale = getRequestLocale(context) ?? 'en-US';
  const serialized = serializeI18nError(i18n, error, locale);
  return new ApiError({
    status: apiErrorStatusFromHttp(error.status),
    reason: serialized.code,
    domain: 'orders',
    message: error.message,
    localizedMessage: { locale, message: serialized.message },
    metadata: {
      ns: serialized.ns,
      key: serialized.key,
      ...(serialized.params ? { params: serialized.params } : {}),
    },
    cause: error,
  });
}

router.onError((error, context) =>
  apiErrorHandler(
    isAppI18nError(error) ? toOrdersApiError(error, context) : error,
    context,
  ),
);
```

        原文件 SHA-256：48013bdbf9a871bde0c159293d1d5804fe5bc8e7384b70bbd9cc9a3308351f8d

        E12 · packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-i18n/dist/client/use-locale.js:19–55

        useAppLocale 暴露 locale、availableLocales、加载状态和 setLocale，切换时先等待资源加载再更新运行时。

        /**
 * The current language and a way to change it.
 *
 * Loads and switches the interface before persisting the choice and notifying the server. A server fallback is a
 * successful result the control can explain; transport failures reject without undoing the browser's language.
 */
export function useAppLocale() {
    const api = useApiClient();
    const runtime = useOptionalI18nRuntime();
    const { locale, locales, setLocale, switching, error } = useRuntimeLocale();
    const [syncing, setSyncing] = useState(false);
    const [syncError, setSyncError] = useState();
    const changeLocale = useCallback(async (next) => {
        setSyncing(true);
        setSyncError(undefined);
        try {
            await setLocale(next);
            const selected = runtime?.getLocale() ?? next;
            writeStoredLocale(selected);
            return await notifyServerLocale(api, selected);
        }
        catch (cause) {
            setSyncError(cause instanceof Error ? cause : new Error(String(cause)));
            throw cause;
        }
        finally {
            setSyncing(false);
        }
    }, [api, runtime, setLocale]);
    return {
        locale,
        locales,
        setLocale: changeLocale,
        switching: switching || syncing,
        error: syncError ?? error,
    };
}

        原文件 SHA-256：4ac6a653f32beb82cf7bc0ecae597598c004f1703994faabb04019635eeee100

        E13 · packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-i18n/dist/client/use-locale.js:56–76

        setLocale 成功后写入浏览器存储并 PUT i18n/locale；同步失败只记录错误，不回滚已选语言。

        /**
 * Tells the server which language this browser is using, once, at startup.
 *
 * Storage and the server's session drift apart routinely — a different browser, an expired session, a switch made in
 * another tab — and this is what brings them back together.
 */
export function useSyncServerLocale() {
    const api = useApiClient();
    // Optional, so the shell can call this unconditionally: an application composed without i18n, which is what a
    // focused test renders, has no language to report and simply does nothing.
    const runtime = useOptionalI18nRuntime();
    useEffect(() => {
        if (!runtime)
            return;
        const synchronization = notifyServerLocale(api, runtime.getLocale());
        synchronization.catch(() => {
            // Nothing to do: the interface is already correct, and the next startup tries again.
        });
    }, [api, runtime]);
}
//# sourceMappingURL=use-locale.js.map

        原文件 SHA-256：4ac6a653f32beb82cf7bc0ecae597598c004f1703994faabb04019635eeee100

        E14 · packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-i18n/README.md:62–79

        README 说明语言选择使用 useAppLocale，并列出 nocobase.locale 持久化和默认 en-US。

        ## What a switch does

`setLocale(next)` runs the whole chain:

1. Loads every registered namespace's resources for the new language, in parallel
2. Changes the interface language, which i18next broadcasts; the application runtime updates `<html lang>` and `<html dir>`
3. Writes `localStorage`, so a refresh cannot lose the choice
4. Tells the server which language was selected and returns its result

Step 1 waits for every namespace together, so the interface switch is atomic: no frame renders half-translated. **Plugins need no code to follow a switch** — one i18next instance serves every namespace, and `useTranslation` subscribes to it.

The interface remains in the selected language when the server does not support it. The server stores `en-US` and returns `fallback: true`; the template shows an informational toast using its client locale messages. An unsupported locale is a normal result, not an HTTP error. Transport failures reject `setLocale` and populate `error` without undoing the browser's choice, so a custom control must handle the rejection and tell the user that server synchronization failed.

## Keeping the server in step

The browser is the source of truth for what it renders; the server keeps its own copy so it can answer in the right language. They drift apart routinely — a different browser, an expired session, a switch made in another tab.

`useSyncServerLocale()` sends the browser's language once at startup to reconcile them. It does not block rendering.

        原文件 SHA-256：6c3231dfbe6fa746300cc5954b586e0a4785354ee8ac30ed1715fefb22c2ccbc

        E15 · packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-i18n/README.md:81–93

        README 说明服务端偏好通过 i18n/locale 同步，并提示同一浏览器多标签共享存储的限制。

        ## Endpoints

```
GET /api/i18n/locales   → { data: { defaultLocale, locales: [{ locale, label, direction }] } }
PUT /api/i18n/locale    { locale } → { data: { locale, requestedLocale, fallback } }
```

Both sit under the application's base path, so an application served from `/main` answers at `/main/api/i18n/locale`. The session's language is a singleton setting, so it is replaced with `PUT`. It stores the requested language when supported and otherwise stores English (`en-US`), even if the configured default is another language; that fallback is a successful answer with `fallback: true`, not an error. A missing, empty or non-string `locale`, or any other body field, is answered `400` with reason `INVALID_INPUT` and a field violation in the standard error body.

`GET /api/i18n/locales` is listed in the application's API document at `/api/swagger/docs` under the `I18n` tag (`i18nListLocales`). `PUT /api/i18n/locale` is hidden from it: it stores a preference on the browser's cookie session for the application shell, and a caller with an API key has no session for it to change. What the server offers follows from the application's `server/locales/`; keeping it aligned with `client/locales/` is recommended, but a client-only language is allowed and does not prevent switching.

**A known limit:** the language is stored on the session, so tabs sharing an account overwrite each other. Tab A switching to Chinese means tab B's requests also come back in Chinese while its interface is still English. Error responses carry a stable `reason` alongside the translated `localizedMessage`, so a frontend can render errors in its own interface language from the reason regardless of what the session says.

        E16 · packages/@nocobase/app-client/dist/i18n.js:1–26

        客户端定义 LOCALE_STORAGE_KEY、DEFAULT_LOCALE，并对 localStorage 访问做保护和异常处理。

        import { I18nRuntime } from '@nocobase/i18n';
const LOCALE_STORAGE_KEY = 'nocobase.locale';
/** The language an application falls back to when its configuration names none. */
export const DEFAULT_LOCALE = 'en-US';
/**
 * Reads the visitor's stored language preference.
 *
 * Storage is the source of truth on the client, so the first frame renders in the right language without waiting for
 * the server. Access is guarded because a browser can refuse it outright in private modes.
 */
export function readStoredLocale() {
    try {
        return globalThis.localStorage?.getItem(LOCALE_STORAGE_KEY) ?? undefined;
    }
    catch {
        return undefined;
    }
}
export function writeStoredLocale(locale) {
    try {
        globalThis.localStorage?.setItem(LOCALE_STORAGE_KEY, locale);
    }
    catch {
        // A visitor who blocks storage still gets a working switch for this session.
    }
}

        E17 · packages/@nocobase/app-client/dist/i18n.js:27–53

        客户端运行时从存储恢复语言，创建 Provider 所需的 i18n 实例并暴露当前语言。

        /**
 * Builds the i18n runtime for an application and loads the starting locale.
 *
 * Every contribution's resources for that one locale are fetched in parallel before this resolves, so the application
 * renders already translated instead of flashing keys behind a loading state. Other locales stay unfetched until the
 * visitor switches to one.
 */
export async function createAppI18nRuntime(options) {
    const applicationContribution = options.contributions.find((contribution) => contribution.source === 'application');
    const runtime = new I18nRuntime({
        defaultLocale: options.defaultLocale ?? DEFAULT_LOCALE,
        locales: options.locales,
        applicationNamespace: applicationContribution?.packageName,
    });
    for (const contribution of options.contributions) {
        if (contribution.source === 'application') {
            // Registering the application's namespace is also what settles which languages are on offer: its own locale
            // files are the list, and a plugin's only supply translations for languages already on it.
            runtime.registerApplicationNamespace(contribution.packageName, contribution.locales);
        }
        else {
            runtime.registerNamespace(contribution.packageName, contribution.locales);
        }
    }
    await runtime.init(options.initialLocale ?? readStoredLocale());
    return runtime;
}

        E18 · packages/@nocobase/app-client/dist/runtime/index.js:72–99

        应用启动阶段预加载全部语言贡献，并在渲染主界面前等待资源就绪。

        /** Loads the application's and the plugins' locales and builds the i18n runtime the application starts in. */
async function createI18nRuntime(definition, config) {
    const localeContributions = await collectLocaleContributions(definition);
    const applicationLocales = localeContributions
        .filter(({ source }) => source === 'application')
        .flatMap(({ locales }) => Object.keys('default' in locales ? locales.default : locales));
    // The server publishes the locale it starts in; a `client.i18n.defaultLocale` is the fallback for a page served
    // without it.
    const configuredLocale = config.public.has('i18n.defaultLocale')
        ? config.public.get('i18n.defaultLocale')
        : config.get('i18n.defaultLocale');
    const defaultLocale = (typeof configuredLocale === 'string'
        ? resolveSupportedLocale(configuredLocale, [
            DEFAULT_LOCALE,
            ...applicationLocales,
        ])
        : undefined) ?? DEFAULT_LOCALE;
    const supportedLocales = [...applicationLocales, defaultLocale];
    const storedLocale = readStoredLocale();
    const initialLocale = (storedLocale === undefined
        ? undefined
        : resolveSupportedLocale(storedLocale, supportedLocales)) ??
        defaultLocale;
    return createAppI18nRuntime({
        contributions: localeContributions,
        defaultLocale,
        initialLocale,
    });

        E19 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-i18n/SKILL.md:28–32

        Skill 把“不要自建语言选择器，使用 useAppLocale()”列为明确非目标。

        # Non-Goals

- Do not build a language picker. `useAppLocale()` exists; `app-template-default` already renders one in `client/layouts/components/language-switcher.tsx`.
- Do not add i18n machinery to an application. The plugin is registered by default, the client runtime is built by `createAppI18nRuntime`, and the server's by `I18nProvider`.
- Do not configure the list of languages. It is whatever the application's own `locales/index.ts` declares; configuration names only the default, `i18n.defaultLocale` (overridden by `APP_DEFAULT_LOCALE`). The `@nocobase/app-plugin-i18n` README covers that, `useAppLocale()`, the endpoints, and `useSyncServerLocale()`, which the template shell already calls to keep the server's language in step with the browser's.

        E21 · packages/@nocobase/app-server/dist/router/api-error.js:34–71

        ApiError 公开 code、status、reason、domain、localizedMessage、fieldViolations 和 metadata 字段。

        export class ApiError extends Error {
    /** The HTTP status of the response. */
    code;
    status;
    reason;
    domain;
    localizedMessage;
    fieldViolations;
    metadata;
    constructor(options) {
        super(options.message, options.cause === undefined ? undefined : { cause: options.cause });
        this.name = 'ApiError';
        this.code = options.httpStatus ?? apiErrorStatusCodes[options.status];
        this.status = options.status;
        this.reason = options.reason;
        this.domain = options.domain;
        this.localizedMessage = options.localizedMessage;
        this.fieldViolations = options.fieldViolations;
        this.metadata = options.metadata;
    }
    toPayload(requestId) {
        return {
            code: this.code,
            status: this.status,
            reason: this.reason,
            domain: this.domain,
            message: this.message,
            ...(this.localizedMessage
                ? { localizedMessage: this.localizedMessage }
                : {}),
            ...(this.fieldViolations?.length
                ? { fieldViolations: this.fieldViolations }
                : {}),
            ...(this.metadata ? { metadata: this.metadata } : {}),
            ...(requestId ? { requestId } : {}),
        };
    }
}

        E22 · packages/@nocobase/app-server/dist/router/api-error.js:89–127

        recognizeApiError 依次识别 ApiError、HTTPException、RepositoryError 和带 4xx 状态的错误。

        /**
 * The `ApiError` for an error the framework recognizes, or `undefined` for one it does not:
 *
 * - an `ApiError` as it is;
 * - Hono's `HTTPException`, and any error that carries a 4xx `status` the way Hono's `getResponse()` convention does
 *   (such as `AuthorizationDeniedError`), with their status and message, and a string `reason` and `domain` on such an
 *   error kept too;
 * - a `RepositoryError` whose `status` is not `INTERNAL` (a refused write, a missing record, a version conflict), with
 *   that status, its code as `reason`, domain `app`, and its `path` and `details` as `metadata`. One that is the
 *   server's own fault, such as an invalid Policy, is not recognized.
 */
export function recognizeApiError(error) {
    if (error instanceof ApiError)
        return error;
    if (error instanceof HTTPException) {
        return new ApiError({
            status: apiErrorStatusFromHttp(error.status),
            reason: `HTTP_${error.status}`,
            domain: appErrorDomain,
            message: error.message || `Request failed with status ${error.status}.`,
            httpStatus: error.status,
            cause: error,
        });
    }
    const repositoryError = repositoryApiError(error);
    if (repositoryError)
        return repositoryError;
    const statusError = readStatusError(error);
    if (statusError) {
        return new ApiError({
            status: apiErrorStatusFromHttp(statusError.status),
            reason: statusError.reason ?? `HTTP_${statusError.status}`,
            domain: statusError.domain ?? appErrorDomain,
            message: statusError.message,
            httpStatus: statusError.status,
            cause: error,
        });
    }
    return undefined;

        E23 · packages/@nocobase/app-server/dist/router/api-error.js:143–178

        readStatusError 只按 status 生成 HTTP_ 前缀 reason，未保留 AppI18nError 的 ns/key/params。

        function readStatusError(error) {
    if (!(error instanceof Error) || !('status' in error))
        return undefined;
    const { status } = error;
    if (typeof status !== 'number' || status < 400 || status > 499)
        return undefined;
    const fields = error;
    return {
        status,
        message: error.message,
        ...(typeof fields.reason === 'string' ? { reason: fields.reason } : {}),
        ...(typeof fields.domain === 'string' ? { domain: fields.domain } : {}),
    };
}
export function apiErrorBody(error, requestId) {
    return { error: error.toPayload(requestId) };
}
/**
 * Answer with the standard error body for `error`, carrying the request's id; anything unrecognized is an opaque 500.
 * Framework-internal: plugins answer through `apiErrorHandler`, which rethrows what it does not recognize.
 */
function apiErrorResponse(context, error) {
    const apiError = toApiError(error);
    return context.json(apiErrorBody(apiError, getRequestId(context)), apiError.code);
}
/**
 * The `onError` for a plugin's router: it answers every error `recognizeApiError` recognizes in the standard body and
 * rethrows the rest, so an enclosing router can still translate it and the application answers an unexpected failure
 * with an opaque 500. A router that translates its own domain errors does that first and then delegates here. Install
 * it even though the application renders the same errors: a router tested on a bare Hono has no `/api` handler.
 */
export function apiErrorHandler(error, context) {
    const known = recognizeApiError(error);
    if (known)
        return apiErrorResponse(context, known);
    throw error;

        E24 · packages/@nocobase/i18n/dist/server/errors.js:1–42

        AppI18nError 保存 code、ns、key、params、status，并可由 serializeI18nError 生成带 localizedMessage 的载荷。

        /**
 * An error that carries what it needs to be translated, rather than a message translated when it was thrown.
 *
 * Translation happens at serialization, where the request's locale is known. The same error therefore renders in
 * whatever language the caller asked for, and constructing one never requires knowing the locale.
 */
export class AppI18nError extends Error {
    code;
    ns;
    key;
    params;
    status;
    constructor(code, options) {
        // The untranslated message is what logs and stack traces show; the translated one is produced on serialization.
        super(`${code}: ${options.ns}:${options.key}`, { cause: options.cause });
        this.name = 'AppI18nError';
        this.code = code;
        this.ns = options.ns;
        this.key = options.key;
        this.params = options.params;
        this.status = options.status ?? 400;
    }
}
export function isAppI18nError(error) {
    return error instanceof AppI18nError;
}
/**
 * Renders an error for a response body.
 *
 * `message` is translated into the request's locale so an API-only application needs nothing else, while `ns`, `key`,
 * and `params` let a frontend re-render it in whatever language its interface is currently showing.
 */
export function serializeI18nError(runtime, error, locale) {
    const translate = runtime.getFixedT(error.ns, locale);
    return {
        code: error.code,
        message: translate(error.key, error.params),
        ns: error.ns,
        key: error.key,
        ...(error.params ? { params: error.params } : {}),
    };
}
