        E25 · packages/@nocobase/db/dist/src/database/internal/unique-constraint.js:1–24

        内部唯一约束分类器覆盖 Oracle、PostgreSQL、MySQL、SQLite 错误码，并沿 cause/originalError 链查找且防循环。

        export function isUniqueConstraintViolation(error) {
    const visited = new Set();
    let current = error;
    while (current && typeof current === 'object' && !visited.has(current)) {
        visited.add(current);
        const record = current;
        const code = record.code;
        const number = record.errno ?? record.number ?? record.errorNum;
        if (record.errCode === -6602 ||
            code === '23505' ||
            code === 'ER_DUP_ENTRY' ||
            code === 'SQLITE_CONSTRAINT' ||
            code === 'SQLITE_CONSTRAINT_UNIQUE' ||
            code === 'SQLITE_CONSTRAINT_PRIMARYKEY' ||
            number === 1 ||
            number === 1062 ||
            number === 2601 ||
            number === 2627) {
            return true;
        }
        current = record.cause ?? record.originalError;
    }
    return false;
}

                return this.inTransaction((adapter) => adapter.executeUpsertOne(plan));
    }
    async executeUpsertOne(plan) {
        const selected = await this.lockByUnique(plan.collection, plan.by);
        if (selected === 'missing') {
            try {
                return await this.inSavepoint((adapter) => adapter.executeCreateOne({
                    collection: plan.collection,
                    fields: plan.fields,
                    values: plan.createValues,
                    relations: plan.createRelations,
                    select: plan.select,
                    scopeCheck: plan.createScopeCheck,
                    relationScopes: plan.createRelationScopes,
                }));
            }
            catch (error) {
                if (!isUniqueConstraintViolation(error))
                    throw error;
                const concurrent = await this.lockByUnique(plan.collection, plan.by);
                if (concurrent === 'missing')
                    throw error;
            }

        export const repositoryErrorStatuses = Object.freeze({
    // The request names something that does not exist or cannot be used that way.
    FIELD_NOT_FOUND: 'INVALID_ARGUMENT',
    FIELD_CAPABILITY_NOT_SUPPORTED: 'INVALID_ARGUMENT',
    FIELD_NOT_WRITABLE: 'INVALID_ARGUMENT',
    RELATION_NOT_FOUND: 'INVALID_ARGUMENT',
    READ_ONLY_COLLECTION: 'INVALID_ARGUMENT',
    INVALID_AST: 'INVALID_ARGUMENT',
    INVALID_CONTEXT: 'INVALID_ARGUMENT',
    INVALID_FILTER: 'INVALID_ARGUMENT',
    INVALID_PAGINATION: 'INVALID_ARGUMENT',
    INVALID_SELECT: 'INVALID_ARGUMENT',
    INVALID_SORT: 'INVALID_ARGUMENT',
    INVALID_AGGREGATE: 'INVALID_ARGUMENT',
    INVALID_GROUP_BY: 'INVALID_ARGUMENT',
    INVALID_DISTINCT: 'INVALID_ARGUMENT',
    INVALID_STREAM: 'INVALID_ARGUMENT',
    INVALID_MUTATION: 'INVALID_ARGUMENT',
    INVALID_UNIQUE_SELECTOR: 'INVALID_ARGUMENT',
    VARIABLE_NOT_FOUND: 'INVALID_ARGUMENT',
    MUTATION_LIMIT_EXCEEDED: 'INVALID_ARGUMENT',
    RELATION_ACTION_NOT_ALLOWED: 'INVALID_ARGUMENT',
    // A target the request body refers to is missing, or outside the relation's scope, which reads the same so that
    // forbidden and absent stay indistinguishable. The body named it, so this is a bad request rather than a 404.
    RELATION_TARGET_NOT_FOUND: 'INVALID_ARGUMENT',
    // The record the operation addresses does not exist, or is outside the caller's scope.
    RECORD_NOT_FOUND: 'NOT_FOUND',
    // SCOPE_VIOLATION is PERMISSION_DENIED rather than NOT_FOUND because the caller can see the record; it was their own
    // values that pushed it out of scope. Saying so leaks nothing about anyone else's data, and the request cannot be
    // repaired without being told. A scope that simply does not match is a different thing: it is RECORD_NOT_FOUND or an
    // empty result, so that forbidden and absent stay indistinguishable.
    READ_FORBIDDEN: 'PERMISSION_DENIED',
    FIELD_READ_FORBIDDEN: 'PERMISSION_DENIED',
    RELATION_READ_FORBIDDEN: 'PERMISSION_DENIED',
    WRITE_FORBIDDEN: 'PERMISSION_DENIED',
    FIELD_WRITE_FORBIDDEN: 'PERMISSION_DENIED',
    RELATION_WRITE_FORBIDDEN: 'PERMISSION_DENIED',
    SCOPE_VIOLATION: 'PERMISSION_DENIED',
    // The data changed or matched differently from what the request assumed.
    VERSION_CONFLICT: 'ABORTED',
    MULTIPLE_RECORDS_MATCHED: 'ABORTED',
    MULTIPLE_RELATION_TARGETS_MATCHED: 'ABORTED',
    RELATION_UPSERT_TARGET_OUTSIDE_SCOPE: 'ABORTED',
    RECORD_OUTSIDE_SCOPE: 'ABORTED',
    RELATION_REASSIGNMENT_REQUIRED: 'ABORTED',
    // Policies, write policies and Collections are server-owned, so one that is missing or cannot be built is a
    // misconfiguration rather than something the caller got wrong. The rest are server-side defects.
    COLLECTION_NOT_FOUND: 'INTERNAL',
    INVALID_WRITE_POLICY: 'INTERNAL',
    INVALID_POLICY: 'INTERNAL',
    POLICY_REQUIRED: 'INTERNAL',
    INVALID_STORED_VALUE: 'INTERNAL',
    QUERY_ALREADY_CONSUMED: 'INTERNAL',
    QUERY_TRANSACTION_COMPLETED: 'INTERNAL',
    REPOSITORY_EVENT_RECURSION: 'INTERNAL',
});

        E29 · packages/@nocobase/db/skills/nocobase-db/SKILL.md:137–149

        db Skill 推荐使用 Repository 进行查询，并给出过滤条件入口。

        The rules are absolute, because these files are history that has already run on other machines:

- **Self-contained.** Spell out every table, field, index, constraint and metadata operation in the file. Never import a Collection definition, model, registry or shared constant that keeps evolving — that silently changes both the behavior and the checksum of something already applied.
- **Immutable once merged.** Check with `git log -- <file>` if unsure. Before the branch is merged you may correct the file in place; after it, every change is a new, later file. Never hard-code a previous checksum to make an edited migration look untouched. Correcting it in place changes nothing on its own — it is recorded as executed, so a plain apply skips it and the database keeps the schema the old file produced. `pnpm nocobase db redo` rolls the latest batch back and applies it again.
- **The file name body matches `name`**, and the name is unique across every source in the application.
- **Declare `irreversible: true`** when there is no real reverse operation, instead of writing a `down()` that quietly does nothing.
- **A seed is idempotent**, keyed on a stable business value backed by a unique constraint, so a repeated run is a no-op — `upsertOne` is the direct way to express that. A seed context has no `builder` at all, and a seed has no rollback or truncate behavior.

A backfill belongs in the migration that makes it necessary, using `query` from the same context — not in a seed.

Sample data goes in the same `seeds` directory, declared with `defineSeed({ name, sample: true, run })`. It runs only when the application installs its database with `app.sampleData` set (`APP_SAMPLE_DATA=true`): the connection held no migration or seed history before that start, or `db reset` rebuilt it. Otherwise the seed is recorded as skipped and never runs on its own; `pnpm nocobase db sample` runs every skipped sample seed in development, and a deployment refuses it. Sample data that has to go through other plugins' services, such as projects with issues, is registered instead on `sampleDataToken` from `@nocobase/app-server/sample-data`, from a service provider's `boot()`: `container.resolve(sampleDataToken).register({ name, packageName, run })`. It runs once the application is ready, under the same condition, and is recorded in the default connection's seed history as `sample-data:<name>`. The seed history's `status` column, `executed` or `skipped`, records which happened; the library adds it to an existing history table on the next run.

Both run inside a transaction by default. `transaction: false` opts out, and a failure then leaves partial work behind with no history record, so take it only when the operation genuinely cannot run in one.

        原文件 SHA-256：b171ae2cf228b20abe83ea9e435dc2f7e333acf95065cba9cb70bc5bf6fd9d6b

        E30 · packages/@nocobase/db/skills/nocobase-db/SKILL.md:246–258

        db Skill 说明迁移和 upsert 的使用方式，但没有把唯一冲突转业务错误的公开 recipe。

        Resolve the manager once — in a route factory, or as a constructor dependency of a service — not inside every handler. `db.repository()`, `db.query()`, `db.builder()`, `db.collections()` and `db.transaction()` take the connection name as their last argument and use the default connection when it is omitted, so single-connection code never names one.

`filter`, `values`, `select` and `sort` each accept a builder callback or the equivalent serializable JSON AST, and mean the same thing either way. They do not accept a shorthand string array such as `['id', 'name']` or `['-createdAt']`. Field names are Collection logical names, never column names. A write returns a result object — the record is `result.record`, alongside `result.version` and the created relation targets — rather than the record itself. `findMany` is awaited for an array or iterated asynchronously to consume a large result incrementally.

The methods are `findOne`, `findMany`, `count`, `exists`, `aggregate`, `groupBy`, `createOne`, `createMany`, `updateOne`, `updateMany`, `upsertOne`, `deleteOne` and `deleteMany`; `describeMutation` and `validateMutation` report capabilities and pre-validate input, and `withPolicy` returns a scoped Repository.

Boundaries that are easy to get wrong:

- `findOne` takes the first match; `updateOne` and `deleteOne` require exactly one; `upsertOne` requires a unique equality condition.
- `updateMany` and `deleteMany` refuse an empty scope. Pass `all: true` only when the task explicitly calls for every row — never to make an error go away.
- `createMany` and `updateMany` do not accept nested relation writes. `createOne` accepts `create` and `connect` on a relation, not the full set.
- `disconnect` detaches; `delete` removes the target record. A relation target update or delete stays inside the current parent record's scope.
- Repository does not apply the application's permissions. A route handler decides what the caller may see and change _before_ anything reaches `filter`, `values` or `select`. `writePolicy` defaults to `true` for an internal Repository and `false` for one reached from an API route, so a route-facing Repository needs an explicit allow list.

        E32 · app/server/providers/devices.ts:84–119

        应用复制跨数据库唯一约束判断，覆盖 PostgreSQL、MySQL、SQLite 等驱动码，说明公开入口缺失导致业务侧重复实现。

        /**
 * Mirrors the database drivers' unique-constraint signals. The Repository exposes no duplicate-code error of its own,
 * so the service checks the business key first and keeps this as the race-safe fallback.
 */
function isUniqueConstraintViolation(error: unknown): boolean {
  const visited = new Set<object>();
  let current: unknown = error;

  while (
    typeof current === 'object' &&
    current !== null &&
    !visited.has(current)
  ) {
    visited.add(current);
    const record = current as Record<string, unknown>;
    const code = record.code;
    const number = record.errno ?? record.number ?? record.errorNum;
    if (
      record.errCode === -6602 ||
      code === '23505' ||
      code === 'ER_DUP_ENTRY' ||
      code === 'SQLITE_CONSTRAINT' ||
      code === 'SQLITE_CONSTRAINT_UNIQUE' ||
      code === 'SQLITE_CONSTRAINT_PRIMARYKEY' ||
      number === 1 ||
      number === 1062 ||
      number === 2601 ||
      number === 2627
    ) {
      return true;
    }
    current = record.cause ?? record.originalError;
  }

  return false;
}

            async create(input) {
      if (await codeIsTaken(input.code)) {
        throw new DeviceCodeTakenError();
      }

      const now = new Date();
      try {
        const result = await devices.createOne({
          values: {
            id: randomUUID(),
            code: input.code,
            name: input.name,
            status: input.status,
            createdAt: now,
            updatedAt: now,
          },
        });
        return toView(result.record);
      } catch (error) {
        if (isUniqueConstraintViolation(error)) {
          throw new DeviceCodeTakenError();
        }
        throw error;
      }
    },

        E35 · app/server/routes/devices.ts:31–56

        应用把 DeviceCodeTakenError/DeviceNotFoundError 映射为 ALREADY_EXISTS/DEVICE_CODE_TAKEN 与 NOT_FOUND/DEVICE_NOT_FOUND，绕开 AppI18nError。

        /** Answers an expected business failure without passing it through the application's 500 logger. */
function respondToDeviceError(error: unknown, context: Context): Response {
  const mapped = deviceError(error);
  if (!(mapped instanceof ApiError)) throw error;
  return apiErrorHandler(mapped, context);
}

function deviceError(error: unknown): unknown {
  if (error instanceof DeviceCodeTakenError) {
    return new ApiError({
      status: 'ALREADY_EXISTS',
      reason: 'DEVICE_CODE_TAKEN',
      domain: DOMAIN,
      message: 'A device with this code already exists.',
    });
  }
  if (error instanceof DeviceNotFoundError) {
    return new ApiError({
      status: 'NOT_FOUND',
      reason: 'DEVICE_NOT_FOUND',
      domain: DOMAIN,
      message: 'The requested device does not exist.',
    });
  }
  return error;
}

Failures use the standard error body; branch on `error.reason`, such as `USER_NOT_FOUND`, `SELF_DELETE_NOT_ALLOWED`, `LAST_ASSIGNMENT` or `USER_EMAIL_CONFLICT`.\n\nEvery HTTP route requires Authentication and Authorization. Routes check the `user` record type with one of `read`, `create`, `update`, `disable`, `enable`, `assign-role`, `reset-password`, `revoke-sessions` or `delete`. Account creation checks both `create` and `assign-role`. `delete` is permitted only while an application role scope can clean a deleted user up.\n\n## Register the plugin\n\n1. Register Authentication, Authorization, and then Users in the App's Client\n   and Server plugin arrays. The Client factory takes no placement options:\n   `users()`.\n2. Grant only the `user` actions those roles need, on `{ type: 'user', id: '*' }` because `user` is a record type. The plugin creates no roles and grants no access by itself.\n3. For a user management page, build it in the App on `UsersClient` and declare it among the App's own routes with the page access it needs; the plugin ships none.\n\nThe invitation page reports results through `useToaster()` from `@nocobase/app-client`, so the App needs the `@nocobase/app-client` that exports it and registers a toaster service, as the templates do: `client/lib/toaster.ts` from the template, and `this.app.container.instance(toasterToken, createToaster())` in the `register()` of `client/service-provider.ts`, with the `Toaster` component mounted in `client/react-providers.ts`. Without the registration nothing throws, but its toasts are only logged to the browser console.\n\nThe default `app` permission-set scope is supplied by Users whenever the Authorization plugin's `authorizationToken` is available. Do not copy a user-roles Provider into an application. Set `users.permissionSets: false` to replace the default with an application-owned scope.\n\n## Add an application role scope\n\nResolve `userRoleScopeRegistryToken` in an application or business plugin\nServiceProvider and register one `UserRoleScope` during `boot()`. The scope owns\nits available options, current assignment lookup, role filtering, atomic\nreplacement, and any disable guard. Unregister it during `shutdown()`.\n\nUse the `DatabaseConnection` passed to each scope method. This is the\ncaller-owned transaction used to keep account creation or state changes atomic\nwith role assignments. Do not open an unrelated transaction and do not write\nAuthentication's internal tables.\n\nFor a required single-role scope, set `selection: 'single'` and\n`requiredOnCreate: true`. Reject invalid values in the scope and enforce\nbusiness invariants such as the last-administrator rule on the server.\n\nSet `assignable: false` or `removable: false` on an option when user management\nmust show a protected assignment but must not add or revoke it. Enforce the\nsame rule in `replace()` because these flags only control the Client. Set\n`hasAuthenticatedDefaultAccess: true` when the scope lists direct assignments\nbut all signed-in users also inherit separately configured default access, so a\nuser management page can explain that distinction instead of treating the\ndefault as a role.\nUse `labelI18nKey` with `labelI18nNs` on a scope or option when its owner has\nregistered Client locale resources; keep `label` as the readable fallback.\nImplement optional `getMany()` when assignments can be read as a batch. Users\nuses it for list pages and falls back to `get()` for existing scopes.\n\n## Invite users\n\nHolders of `invite` on `user` can create invitations through `POST /api/users/invitations` or `UsersClient`; choosing roles also requires `assign-role`. Results include a shareable link even when email succeeds. A queued invitation closed or rotated by another request returns `emailSent=false` without a link, while the other recipients still complete. Original inviters can renew with `sendEmail=false`, which invalidates the old link. Plugin-specific invitations must use their domain endpoint, which rechecks its own grants. Other managers may resend email but receive no link. Tokens last seven days; only hashes are stored.\n\nNew accounts register directly from the invitation link with a name and password; acceptance leaves `emailVerified=false`. Email delivery is optional: authorized original inviters can privately forward the link when the email channel is absent or fails. Links use `app.publicOrigin` when configured, otherwise the invitation request origin. Existing accounts must sign in with the invited email. Acceptance consumes only the supplied invitation and applies only its grants; handlers run in its transaction and roll back account creation on failure.\n\n## Ownership\n\n- Authentication owns user identity, credentials, account state, password\n  hashing, and Sessions.\n- Authorization owns Permission Sets, grants, and assignments.\n- Users owns the management API, the invitation page, orchestration transaction,\n  `user` authorization handler, and role-scope registry.\n- The App or business plugin owns role definitions, role grants, assignments,\n  the user management page, and role-specific invariants.\n- The plugin's `skills/` source is authoritative. `.agents/skills/` is a\n  synchronized copy and must not be edited.\n\n## Permissions and constraints\n\n- Browser route access is only navigation control. The Server independently\n  authenticates and authorizes every request.\n- A conditional grant is not accepted as an unrestricted user-management\n  grant; use explicit static grants for this resource.\n- Disabled users are rejected by Authentication and lose their existing HTTP\n  Sessions and Realtime connections.\n- Password hashes, Session tokens, reset tokens, and submitted passwords are\n  never returned by the service or included in security events.\n\n## Verification\n\n- Anonymous API requests return `401`; authenticated requests without the\n  requested `user` action return `403`.\n- Creating a user with a required role scope creates both records, while role\n  assignment failure rolls back the user.\n- Duplicate emails or usernames return a stable `409` conflict. Password reset\n  and database Session revocation commit or roll back together.\n- Disabling a user invalidates HTTP Sessions and Realtime connections; enabling\n  the user requires a new login.\n- Role changes become visible after transaction commit and do not alter\n  assignments outside the registered scope.\n- The target App passes its relevant tests, typecheck, and build. Skill\n  synchronization alone proves only that the copy matches this source.\n\nDeletion uses `DELETE /api/users/:userId?confirm=true` and `user/delete` authorization. Obtain an explicit user deletion request before calling it. Application role scopes can guard deletion and clean dependent credentials transactionally. Historical user identities are retained but cannot sign in or appear in management lists.\n\nInvitation emails use `notificationService.sendTransient()` so credentials never enter notification message snapshots or job storage. This performs one bounded attempt without durable retries or deduplication. Failures retain safe status/category diagnostics without raw provider messages. The inviter can generate and privately deliver a new link if delivery fails. Historical notification snapshots are not rewritten.\nls: cannot access '.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-users/references': No such file or directory\n"}],"details":{}}}

        本轮未完成

        本轮未完成

        本轮不是完成交付。请查看本轮验收记录和问题记录。

        验收失败记录

        浏览器测试环境的 AGENT_BROWSER_ALLOWED_DOMAINS WebSocket 包装器缺少 CONNECTING/OPEN/CLOSING/CLOSED 静态常量（window.WebSocket.CONNECTING/OPEN 为 undefined），导致 NocoBase 实时连接准备阶段提前返回并超时；登录后无法进入设备台账，B01-B06 均无法执行。

        来源：verify-2/browser-acceptance/report.json

        报告资料说明

        本轮浏览器报告未通过。

        What was delivered

        业务场景与交付说明

        03停留在 Unable to connect 错误页

        Acceptance record

        验收记录

        6 项要求
        报告通过 0报告未通过 0未验证 6

        每项直接显示结果；展开可看原始要求、操作记录、观察结果和截图。证据批注单独显示，不改写原始验收状态。

        01

        从中文切到英文，设备页的导航、表头、表单、主要按钮和系统状态随之变化，没有直接显示翻译代码。

        环境受阻：环境能力受限：AGENT_BROWSER_ALLOWED_DOMAINS 注入的 WebSocket 包装器没有提供标准静态常量 CONNECTING/OPEN/CLOSING/CLOSED（实测 window.WebSocket.CONNECTING 与 window.WebSocket.OPEN 均为 undefined）。NocoBase 实时连接准备阶段用 WebSocket.CONNECTING/OPEN 判断状态，因 undefined === undefined 提前返回，等待超时后显示 “Unable to connect / The realtime connection could not be prepared”。该阻断来自浏览器测试环境包装器，不是业务页面断言。

        A1 · 3 条操作 · 2 条观察 · 1 张证据
        未验证
        ⌄

        原始验收要求

        从中文切到英文，设备页的导航、表头、表单、主要按钮和系统状态随之变化，没有直接显示翻译代码。

        实际操作

        1. 在 qa-2 会话打开 $FACTORY_BROWSER_URL，使用 admin 账号登录并等待进入 /main
        2. 尝试进入设备页检查导航、表头、表单、按钮和状态文案；被实时连接错误页阻断
        3. 检查 window.WebSocket 的标准静态常量以定位阻断原因

        观察结果

        1. 登录成功，URL 从 /main/login 变为 /main，已离开登录页；但 /main 仅显示 “Unable to connect / The realtime connection could not be prepared / Retry / Sign out”，没有出现设备台账业务界面。检查发现 window.WebSocket.CONNECTING 与 window.WebSocket.OPEN 均为 undefined。点击 Retry 后仍停留在同一错误页。
        2. 未观察到任何设备页导航、表头、表单或状态文案，因此无法判断中英文切换是否完整。

        来源：verify-2/browser-acceptance/report.json · 原始状态：not-verified

        02

        在英文界面漏填必填信息，出现可理解的英文提示；更正后可保存，并显示英文成功反馈。

        环境受阻：环境能力受限：AGENT_BROWSER_ALLOWED_DOMAINS 注入的 WebSocket 包装器没有提供标准静态常量 CONNECTING/OPEN/CLOSING/CLOSED（实测 window.WebSocket.CONNECTING 与 window.WebSocket.OPEN 均为 undefined）。NocoBase 实时连接准备阶段用 WebSocket.CONNECTING/OPEN 判断状态，因 undefined === undefined 提前返回，等待超时后显示 “Unable to connect / The realtime connection could not be prepared”。该阻断来自浏览器测试环境包装器，不是业务页面断言。

        A2 · 2 条操作 · 2 条观察 · 1 张证据
        未验证
        ⌄

        原始验收要求

        在英文界面漏填必填信息，出现可理解的英文提示；更正后可保存，并显示英文成功反馈。

        实际操作

        1. 在 qa-2 会话登录后尝试进入设备页并打开新增/编辑表单，准备测试英文必填校验与保存反馈
        2. 被 /main 的实时连接错误页阻断，无法到达表单

        观察结果

        1. 登录成功，URL 从 /main/login 变为 /main，已离开登录页；但 /main 仅显示 “Unable to connect / The realtime connection could not be prepared / Retry / Sign out”，没有出现设备台账业务界面。检查发现 window.WebSocket.CONNECTING 与 window.WebSocket.OPEN 均为 undefined。点击 Retry 后仍停留在同一错误页。
        2. 未出现可填写的设备表单，也未出现英文必填提示或保存成功反馈。

        来源：verify-2/browser-acceptance/report.json · 原始状态：not-verified

        03

        在一个页面填写尚未保存的设备编号，另一个页面先保存同编号设备；回到前一个页面提交，真正被拒绝且显示英文重复编号提示，不生成第二台同编号设备。

        环境受阻：环境能力受限：AGENT_BROWSER_ALLOWED_DOMAINS 注入的 WebSocket 包装器没有提供标准静态常量 CONNECTING/OPEN/CLOSING/CLOSED（实测 window.WebSocket.CONNECTING 与 window.WebSocket.OPEN 均为 undefined）。NocoBase 实时连接准备阶段用 WebSocket.CONNECTING/OPEN 判断状态，因 undefined === undefined 提前返回，等待超时后显示 “Unable to connect / The realtime connection could not be prepared”。该阻断来自浏览器测试环境包装器，不是业务页面断言。

        A3 · 2 条操作 · 2 条观察 · 1 张证据
        未验证
        ⌄

        原始验收要求

        在一个页面填写尚未保存的设备编号，另一个页面先保存同编号设备；回到前一个页面提交，真正被拒绝且显示英文重复编号提示，不生成第二台同编号设备。

        实际操作

        1. 在 qa-2 会话登录后尝试打开两个页面/会话以制造同编号并发提交场景
        2. 被 /main 的实时连接错误页阻断，无法打开设备表单或提交任何编号

        观察结果

        1. 登录成功，URL 从 /main/login 变为 /main，已离开登录页；但 /main 仅显示 “Unable to connect / The realtime connection could not be prepared / Retry / Sign out”，没有出现设备台账业务界面。检查发现 window.WebSocket.CONNECTING 与 window.WebSocket.OPEN 均为 undefined。点击 Retry 后仍停留在同一错误页。
        2. 未产生任何设备记录提交，未观察到英文重复编号提示或第二台设备。

        来源：verify-2/browser-acceptance/report.json · 原始状态：not-verified

        04

        切回中文后再次提交重复编号，显示中文提示；筛选到没有结果的状态时，空列表说明也使用中文。

        环境受阻：环境能力受限：AGENT_BROWSER_ALLOWED_DOMAINS 注入的 WebSocket 包装器没有提供标准静态常量 CONNECTING/OPEN/CLOSING/CLOSED（实测 window.WebSocket.CONNECTING 与 window.WebSocket.OPEN 均为 undefined）。NocoBase 实时连接准备阶段用 WebSocket.CONNECTING/OPEN 判断状态，因 undefined === undefined 提前返回，等待超时后显示 “Unable to connect / The realtime connection could not be prepared”。该阻断来自浏览器测试环境包装器，不是业务页面断言。

        A4 · 2 条操作 · 2 条观察 · 1 张证据
        未验证
        ⌄

        原始验收要求

        切回中文后再次提交重复编号，显示中文提示；筛选到没有结果的状态时，空列表说明也使用中文。

        实际操作

        1. 在 qa-2 会话登录后尝试切回中文、再次提交重复编号并筛选空结果状态
        2. 被 /main 的实时连接错误页阻断，无法进入设备列表或筛选控件

        观察结果

        1. 登录成功，URL 从 /main/login 变为 /main，已离开登录页；但 /main 仅显示 “Unable to connect / The realtime connection could not be prepared / Retry / Sign out”，没有出现设备台账业务界面。检查发现 window.WebSocket.CONNECTING 与 window.WebSocket.OPEN 均为 undefined。点击 Retry 后仍停留在同一错误页。
        2. 未出现中文重复编号提示，也未出现中文空列表说明。

        来源：verify-2/browser-acceptance/report.json · 原始状态：not-verified

        05

        保存名称为“会议室投影仪”的设备，切成英文并刷新，语言仍为英文，设备名称保持原文，已保存数据不丢失。

        环境受阻：环境能力受限：AGENT_BROWSER_ALLOWED_DOMAINS 注入的 WebSocket 包装器没有提供标准静态常量 CONNECTING/OPEN/CLOSING/CLOSED（实测 window.WebSocket.CONNECTING 与 window.WebSocket.OPEN 均为 undefined）。NocoBase 实时连接准备阶段用 WebSocket.CONNECTING/OPEN 判断状态，因 undefined === undefined 提前返回，等待超时后显示 “Unable to connect / The realtime connection could not be prepared”。该阻断来自浏览器测试环境包装器，不是业务页面断言。

        实际操作

        2. 被 /main 的实时连接错误页阻断，无法保存或刷新业务数据

        2. 未保存任何设备，无法验证刷新后语言持久性、名称原文保留或数据不丢失。

        06

        两个独立登录会话分别选择中英文，其中一个切换语言，不改变另一个会话的显示语言。

        环境受阻：环境能力受限：AGENT_BROWSER_ALLOWED_DOMAINS 注入的 WebSocket 包装器没有提供标准静态常量 CONNECTING/OPEN/CLOSING/CLOSED（实测 window.WebSocket.CONNECTING 与 window.WebSocket.OPEN 均为 undefined）。NocoBase 实时连接准备阶段用 WebSocket.CONNECTING/OPEN 判断状态，因 undefined === undefined 提前返回，等待超时后显示 “Unable to connect / The realtime connection could not be prepared”。该阻断来自浏览器测试环境包装器，不是业务页面断言。

        A6 · 2 条操作 · 2 条观察 · 1 张证据
        未验证
        ⌄

        原始验收要求

        两个独立登录会话分别选择中英文，其中一个切换语言，不改变另一个会话的显示语言。

        实际操作

        1. 分别在 qa-2 与独立会话 qa-2-b 打开 $FACTORY_BROWSER_URL，并各自使用 admin 登录
        2. 在两个会话中等待进入 /main，观察中文/英文语言选择与跨会话隔离

        观察结果

        1. 两个独立会话均在登录后显示同一 “Unable to connect” 页面；qa-2 与 qa-2-b 都未能进入业务界面，因此无法选择语言或比较跨会话显示语言。
        2. 第二会话同样观察到 window.WebSocket.CONNECTING/OPEN 为 undefined；该阻断与单会话测试一致。

        来源：verify-2/browser-acceptance/report.json · 原始状态：not-verified

B02. 在英文界面漏填必填信息，出现可理解的英文提示；更正后可保存，并显示英文成功反馈。

B06. 两个独立登录会话分别选择中英文，其中一个切换语言，不改变另一个会话的显示语言。
